@Composable
fun AccentRoute(activity: ComponentActivity, topInset: Dp) {
    val options = remember { AccentPalette.options(activity) }
    AccentColorScreen(
        options = options,
        selected = MainPreferences.getAccentColor(),
        materialYou = MainPreferences.isMaterialYouAccentColor(),
        topInset = topInset,
    ) { option: AccentOption ->
        if (!AccentPalette.isSelected(option, MainPreferences.getAccentColor(), MainPreferences.isMaterialYouAccentColor())) {
            MainPreferences.setMaterialYouAccentColor(option.dynamic)
            MainPreferences.setAccentColor(option.color)
            activity.recreate()
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ArtsRoute() {
    val radius = with(LocalDensity.current) { MainPreferences.getCornerRadius().toDp() }
    ArtsScreen(MainPreferences.getCurrentArt(), radius, LocationPins.getLocationPin(), MainPreferences::setCurrentArt)
}

@Composable
fun TimeZoneRoute(navigator: AppNavigator, topInset: Dp) {
    TimeZoneScreen(ClockPreferences.getTimeZone(), ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat(), LocaleHelper.getAppLocale(), topInset) {
        ClockPreferences.setTimeZone(it)
        navigator.back()
    }
}

@Composable
fun MeasuresRoute(navigator: AppNavigator, topInset: Dp) {
    val viewModel: MeasureViewModel = viewModel()
    val measures by viewModel.measures.collectAsState()
    val records = remember(measures) {
        val selected = MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure()
        measures?.map { Record(it.dateCreated, it, it.name.orEmpty(), it.note.orEmpty(), it.dateCreated, it.dateCreated == selected) }
    }
    val actions = remember(viewModel) {
        object : RecordActions<Measure> {
            override fun open(record: Measure) {
                MeasurePreferences.setLastSelectedMeasure(record.dateCreated)
                navigator.back()
            }

            override fun add(name: String, note: String) {
                viewModel.addMeasure(name, note)
                navigator.back()
            }

            override fun delete(record: Measure) = viewModel.deleteMeasure(record)

            override fun isValidName(name: String) = name.isNotBlank()
        }
    }
    RecordsScreen(stringResource(R.string.saved_measures), records, topInset, actions)
}

@Composable
fun TrailsRoute(navigator: AppNavigator, topInset: Dp) {
    val viewModel: TrailsViewModel = viewModel()
    val trails by viewModel.trails.collectAsState()
    val records = remember(trails) {
        val current = TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail()
        trails?.map { Record(it.keyTag, it, it.trailName.orEmpty(), it.trailNote.orEmpty(), it.dateCreated, it.trailName == current) }
    }
    val actions = remember(viewModel) {
        object : RecordActions<TrailEntry> {
            override fun open(record: TrailEntry) {
                TrailPreferences.setCurrentTrailName(record.trailName)
                navigator.back()
            }

            override fun add(name: String, note: String) = viewModel.addTrail(TrailEntry(System.currentTimeMillis(), name, note))

            override fun delete(record: TrailEntry) = viewModel.deleteTrail(record)

            override fun isValidName(name: String) = TrailNames.isValid(name, viewModel.trails.value.orEmpty().map { it.trailName })
        }
    }
    RecordsScreen(stringResource(R.string.saved_trails), records, topInset, actions)
}

@Composable
fun DirectionsRoute(navigator: AppNavigator, topInset: Dp) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val viewModel: DirectionsViewModel = viewModel()
    val directions by viewModel.directions.collectAsState()
    val actions = remember(viewModel) {
        object : DirectionsActions {
            override fun create() {
                val target = DirectionPreferences.getTargetCoordinates()
                viewModel.open(DirectionPreferences.getTargetLabel(), target[0].toDouble(), target[1].toDouble())
            }

            override fun createFromTarget() {
                val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates()
                viewModel.open(null, target[0].toDouble(), target[1].toDouble())
            }

            override fun set(direction: DirectionModel) = select(direction, useMapsTarget = false)

            override fun edit(direction: DirectionModel) =
                viewModel.open(direction.name, direction.latitude, direction.longitude, direction.dateAdded)

            override fun useAsTarget(direction: DirectionModel) {
                val last = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarker(true)
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLatitude(direction.latitude.toFloat())
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLongitude(direction.longitude.toFloat())
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerStartLatitude(last[0])
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerStartLongitude(last[1])
                select(direction, useMapsTarget = true)
            }

            override fun navigate(direction: DirectionModel) = MapIntents.navigate(context, direction.latitude, direction.longitude)

            override fun delete(direction: DirectionModel) = viewModel.delete(direction)

            override fun search() = navigator.search { viewModel.form.fill(it) }

            override fun save(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) = viewModel.save(latitude, longitude)

            override fun dismissEditor() = viewModel.close()

            private fun select(direction: DirectionModel, useMapsTarget: Boolean) {
                DirectionPreferences.setTargetLatitude(direction.latitude.toFloat())
                DirectionPreferences.setTargetLongitude(direction.longitude.toFloat())
                DirectionPreferences.setTargetLabel(direction.name.orEmpty())
                DirectionPreferences.setUseMapsTarget(useMapsTarget)
                navigator.back()
            }
        }
    }
    DirectionsScreen(directions, viewModel.editing != null, viewModel.form, topInset, actions)
}

@Composable
fun CustomLocationsRoute(navigator: AppNavigator, topInset: Dp) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val viewModel: CustomLocationViewModel = viewModel()
    LaunchedEffect(viewModel) {
        if (viewModel.form.latitude.isEmpty() && MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) {
            val coordinates = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()
            viewModel.form.fill(MainPreferences.getAddress(), coordinates[0].toString(), coordinates[1].toString())
        }
    }
    val locations by viewModel.locations.collectAsState()
    val actions = remember(viewModel) {
        object : CustomLocationActions {
            override fun search() = navigator.search { viewModel.form.fill(it) }

            override fun save(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) = viewModel.save(newLocation(latitude, longitude))

            override fun setLocation(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, save: Boolean) {
                if (save) viewModel.save(newLocation(latitude, longitude))
                use(viewModel.form.address, latitude, longitude)
            }

            override fun select(location: Locations) =
                viewModel.form.fill(location.address, location.latitude.toString(), location.longitude.toString())

            override fun useLocation(location: Locations) = use(location.address, location.latitude, location.longitude)

            override fun delete(location: Locations) = viewModel.delete(location)

            override fun deleteAll() = viewModel.deleteAll()

            override fun help() = navigator.navigate(Routes.web(HtmlPage.CUSTOM_COORDINATES_HELP))

            override fun failed() = Toast.makeText(context, R.string.failed, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()

            private fun newLocation(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) = Locations().apply {
                address = viewModel.form.address.trim().ifEmpty { "----" }.capitalizeText()
                this.latitude = latitude
                this.longitude = longitude
                date = System.currentTimeMillis()
            }

            private fun use(address: String, latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
                MainPreferences.setCustomCoordinates(true)
                MainPreferences.setLatitude(latitude.toFloat())
                MainPreferences.setLongitude(longitude.toFloat())
                MainPreferences.setAddress(address)
                navigator.back()
            }
        }
    }
    CustomLocationScreen(viewModel.form, locations, topInset, actions)
}

@Composable
fun MapSearchRoute(navigator: AppNavigator) {
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val viewModel: MapSearchViewModel = viewModel()
    val suggestions by viewModel.suggestions.collectAsState()
    val mapView = rememberMapView()
    var map by remember { mutableStateOf<GoogleMap?>(null) }
    LaunchedEffect(mapView) {
        mapView.getMapAsync { googleMap ->
            map = googleMap
            googleMap.uiSettings.isMapToolbarEnabled = false
            googleMap.uiSettings.isCompassEnabled = false
            val night = context.resources.configuration.uiMode and Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_MASK == Configuration.UI_MODE_NIGHT_YES
            runCatching {
                googleMap.setMapStyle(MapStyleOptions.loadRawResourceStyle(context, if (night) R.raw.maps_dark_labelled else R.raw.maps_light_labelled))
            }
            CoordinateInput.parse(viewModel.form.latitude, viewModel.form.longitude)?.let { (lat, lon) ->
                googleMap.moveCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newLatLngZoom(LatLng(lat, lon), SEARCH_ZOOM))
            }
            googleMap.setOnCameraIdleListener {
                googleMap.cameraPosition.target.let { viewModel.onCameraIdle(it.latitude, it.longitude) }
            }
            googleMap.setOnMapClickListener { googleMap.animateCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newLatLng(it)) }
        }
    }
    val actions = remember(viewModel) {
        object : MapSearchActions {
            override fun addressChanged(text: String) = viewModel.onAddressChange(text)

            override fun pick(address: Address) {
                viewModel.pick(address)
                map?.animateCamera(CameraUpdateFactory.newLatLngZoom(LatLng(address.latitude, address.longitude), SEARCH_ZOOM))
            }

            override fun cancel() = navigator.back()

            override fun confirm() {
                val form = viewModel.form
                val coordinates = CoordinateInput.parse(form.latitude, form.longitude)
                if (coordinates == null) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, R.string.failed, Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    return
                }
                navigator.deliver(SearchResult(form.address.trim(), coordinates.first, coordinates.second))
                navigator.back()
            }
        }
    }
    MapSearchScreen(viewModel.form, suggestions, mapView, actions)
}

@Composable
fun WebRoute(pageName: String?, navigator: AppNavigator) {
    val page = remember(pageName) { HtmlPage.forName(pageName) }
    if (page == null) {
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) { navigator.back() }
        return
    }
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val webView = remember(page) { CustomWebView(context).apply { loadUrl(page.url) } }
    DisposableEffect(webView) { onDispose { webView.destroy() } }
    Surface(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background) {
        AndroidView(factory = { webView }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize().systemBarsPadding())
    }
}

private fun app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CustomLocationForm.fill(result: SearchResult) = fill(
    address = result.address.ifEmpty { address },
    latitude = result.latitude.toString(),
    longitude = result.longitude.toString(),
)

private const val SEARCH_ZOOM = 15F

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/ClockReport.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import android.content.Context
import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toMiles
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockTime
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SkyReport
import app.simple.positional.util.Direction
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import app.simple.positional.util.MoonAngle
import app.simple.positional.util.MoonTimeFormatter.formatMoonDate
import app.simple.positional.util.Ordinal.toOrdinal
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonIllumination
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonPhase
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonPosition
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonTimes
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunPosition
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZoneOffset
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.time.temporal.IsoFields

class ClockReport(private val context: Context) {
    private fun text(@StringRes id: Int) = context.getString(id)

    private fun format(time: ZonedDateTime, pattern: String) =
        time.format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(pattern).withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()))

    fun time(zone: ZoneId): ClockTime {
        val local = ZonedDateTime.now(zone)
        val utc = local.withZoneSameInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC)
        return ClockTime(
            local = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_timezone), zone.id),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_time_24hr), format(local, "HH:mm:ss")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_time_12hr), format(local, "hh:mm:ss a")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_date), format(local, "dd MMMM, yyyy")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_day), format(local, "EEEE")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_day_of_year), local.dayOfYear.toOrdinal()),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_week_of_year), local.get(IsoFields.WEEK_OF_WEEK_BASED_YEAR).toOrdinal()),
            ),
            utc = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.utc_local_time_offset), format(local, "XXX").replace("Z", "+00:00")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.utc_current_time), format(utc, if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) "hh:mm:ss a" else "HH:mm:ss")),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.local_date), format(utc, "dd MMMM, yyyy")),
            ),
        )
    }

    fun sky(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, altitude: Double, zone: ZoneId): SkyReport {
        val now = Instant.now()
        val timezone = zone.id
        val pattern = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(timePattern()).withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
        val metric = MainPreferences.isMetric()
        val notAvailable = text(R.string.not_available)

        fun time(value: ZonedDateTime?) = value?.format(pattern) ?: notAvailable
        fun range(from: ZonedDateTime?, to: ZonedDateTime?) = "${time(from)} ~ ${time(to)}"
        fun degrees(value: Double) = "${round(value, 2)}°"
        fun direction(azimuth: Double) = "${degrees(azimuth)} ${Direction.getDirectionCodeFromAzimuth(context, azimuth)}"
        fun distance(kilometers: Double) =
            if (metric) "${"%.3E".format(kilometers)} ${text(R.string.kilometer)}" else "${"%.3E".format(kilometers.toMiles())} ${text(R.string.miles)}"

        fun sunTimes() = SunTimes.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).elevation(altitude)
        val sun = sunTimes().execute()
        val twilight = listOf(
            SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL,
            SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON,
            SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL, SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER, SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR,
        ).associateWith { sunTimes().twilight(it).execute() }
        val golden = sunTimes().twilight(GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE).execute()
        fun rise(t: SunTimes.Twilight) = twilight.getValue(t).rise
        fun set(t: SunTimes.Twilight) = twilight.getValue(t).set

        val sunPosition = SunPosition.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).at(latitude, longitude).elevation(altitude).execute()
        val moonPosition = MoonPosition.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).elevation(altitude).execute()
        val moonTimes = MoonTimes.compute().on(now).timezone(timezone).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).elevation(altitude).execute()
        val illumination = MoonIllumination.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).execute()
        fun moonDate(phase: MoonPhase.Phase) = formatMoonDate(MoonPhase.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).phase(phase).execute().time)

        return SkyReport(
            sunPosition = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_azimuth), direction(sunPosition.azimuth)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_altitude), degrees(sunPosition.trueAltitude)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_distance), distance(sunPosition.distance)),
            ),
            sunTime = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_sunrise), time(sun.rise)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_sunset), time(sun.set)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_noon), time(sun.noon)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.sun_nadir), time(sun.nadir)),
            ),
            twilight = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_morning)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_astronomical_dusk), range(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_nautical_dusk), range(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_blue_hour), range(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_civil_dusk), range(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_visual), time(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_horizon), time(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_visual_lower), time(rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_golden_hour), range(golden.rise, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_evening)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_golden_hour), range(set(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR), golden.set)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_visual_lower), time(set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_horizon), time(set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_visual), time(set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_civil_dusk), range(set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON), set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_blue_hour), range(set(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_nautical_dusk), range(set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL))),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.twilight_astronomical_dusk), range(set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL))),
            ),
            moonPosition = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_azimuth), direction(moonPosition.azimuth)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_altitude), degrees(moonPosition.altitude)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.true_altitude), degrees(moonPosition.trueAltitude)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_distance), distance(moonPosition.distance)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_parallactic_angle), degrees(moonPosition.parallacticAngle)),
            ),
            moonTime = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_moonrise), time(moonTimes.rise)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_moonset), time(moonTimes.set)),
            ),
            moonIllumination = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_fraction), round(illumination.fraction, 2).toString()),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_angle), degrees(illumination.angle)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_angle_state), text(if (illumination.angle < 0) R.string.waxing else R.string.waning)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_phase), MoonAngle.getMoonPhase(context, illumination.phase)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_phase_angle), degrees(illumination.phase)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.elongation), distance(illumination.elongation)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.radius), distance(illumination.radius)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.crescent_width), distance(illumination.crescentWidth)),
            ),
            moonDates = listOf(
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_full_moon), moonDate(MoonPhase.Phase.FULL_MOON)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_new_moon), moonDate(MoonPhase.Phase.NEW_MOON)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_first_quarter), moonDate(MoonPhase.Phase.FIRST_QUARTER)),
                InfoRow(text(R.string.moon_last_quarter), moonDate(MoonPhase.Phase.LAST_QUARTER)),
            ),
            sunAzimuth = sunPosition.azimuth.toFloat(),
            moonAzimuth = moonPosition.azimuth.toFloat(),
            moonGraphic = MoonAngle.getMoonPhaseGraphics(round(illumination.phase, 2)),
        )
    }

    private fun timePattern(): String {
        val seconds = ClockPreferences.isUsingSecondsPrecision()
        return when {
            ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat() -> if (seconds) "hh:mm:ss a" else "hh:mm a"
            seconds -> "HH:mm:ss"
            else -> "HH:mm"
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE = -4.0
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Compass.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.hardware.GeomagneticField
import android.hardware.Sensor
import android.location.Location
import android.os.SystemClock
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.CompassBloom.compassBloomRes
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.BloomSkinDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PhysicalPropertiesDialog
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.SensorPanel
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.preferences.CompassPreferences
import app.simple.positional.sensors.HeadingSensor
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CompassScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CompassState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.axisRows
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel

class Compass(activity: ComponentActivity) : SensorPanel(activity), HeadingSensor.Listener {
    private val state = CompassState()
    private val sensor: HeadingSensor
    private var lastReadings = 0L

    init {
        sensor = HeadingSensor(activity, this)
        lifecycle.addObserver(sensor)
        applyPreferences()
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        CompassScreen(state, topInset, onCalibrate = { showCalibration() }, onMenu = ::showMenu)
    }

    private fun showMenu() = menuSheet.show {
        PanelMenus.compass(
            onBloomSkins = {
                dialogs.show { BloomSkinDialog(CompassPreferences.getFlowerBloomTheme(), dialogs::dismiss) { CompassPreferences.setFlowerBloom(it) } }
            },
            onPhysicalProperties = {
                dialogs.show { PhysicalPropertiesDialog(dialogs::dismiss) { openPage(HtmlPage.PHYSICAL_PROPERTIES) } }
            },
        )
    }

    init {
        if (!sensor.isAvailable) showSensorError()
        ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java].getLocation().observe(owner) { showField(it) }
    }

    override fun onHeading(azimuth: Float) = state.onHeading(azimuth)

    override fun onAccuracy(type: Int, accuracy: Int) {
        when (type) {
            Sensor.TYPE_ACCELEROMETER -> state.accelerometerAccuracy = accuracy
            Sensor.TYPE_MAGNETIC_FIELD -> state.magnetometerAccuracy = accuracy
        }
        checkCalibration(type, accuracy)
    }

    override fun onReadings(accelerometer: FloatArray, magnetometer: FloatArray) {
        val now = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        if (now - lastReadings < READINGS_INTERVAL) return
        lastReadings = now
        state.accelerometer = axisRows(accelerometer)
        state.magnetometer = axisRows(magnetometer)
    }

    private fun showField(location: Location) {
        val field = GeomagneticField(location.latitude.toFloat(), location.longitude.toFloat(), location.altitude.toFloat(), location.time)
        state.field = listOf(
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.compass_inclination), "${round(field.inclination.toDouble(), 2)}°"),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.compass_declination), "${round(field.declination.toDouble(), 2)}°"),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.compass_field_strength), "${round(field.fieldStrength.toDouble(), 2)} nT"),
        )
    }

    private fun applyPreferences() {
        sensor.gimbalLock = CompassPreferences.isUsingGimbalLock()
        state.physics = CompassPreferences.isUsingPhysicalProperties()
        state.directionCode = CompassPreferences.getDirectionCode()
        state.bloom = if (CompassPreferences.isFlowerBloomOn()) compassBloomRes.getOrNull(CompassPreferences.getFlowerBloomTheme()) else null
        state.configure(CompassPreferences.getRotationalInertia(), CompassPreferences.getDampingCoefficient(), CompassPreferences.getMagneticCoefficient())
    }

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        if (key in compassKeys) applyPreferences()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val READINGS_INTERVAL = 250L
        private val compassKeys = setOf(
            CompassPreferences.direction_code,
            CompassPreferences.flowerBloom,
            CompassPreferences.flowerBloomTheme,
            CompassPreferences.dampingCoefficient,
            CompassPreferences.magneticCoefficient,
            CompassPreferences.rotationalInertia,
            CompassPreferences.usePhysicalProperties,
            CompassPreferences.useGimbalLock,
        )
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Direction.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.location.Location
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.SensorPanel
import app.simple.positional.preferences.DirectionPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.sensors.HeadingSensor
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.DirectionPanel
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.DirectionState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.util.DMSConverter
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng

class Direction(activity: ComponentActivity) : SensorPanel(activity), HeadingSensor.Listener {
    private val state = DirectionState()
    private val sensor: HeadingSensor
    private var target = LatLng(0.0, 0.0)
    private var location: Location? = null

    init {
        sensor = HeadingSensor(activity, this)
        sensor.gimbalLock = DirectionPreferences.isGimbalLock()
        lifecycle.addObserver(sensor)
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        DirectionPanel(
            state,
            topInset,
            onCalibrate = { showCalibration() },
            onTarget = ::openTargets,
            onMenu = { menuSheet.show { PanelMenus.direction(::openTargets) } },
        )
    }

    private fun openTargets() = navigate(Routes.DIRECTIONS)

    init {
        if (!sensor.isAvailable) showSensorError()
        loadTarget()
        ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java].getLocation().observe(owner) {
            location = it
            updateTarget()
        }
    }

    override fun onHeading(azimuth: Float) = state.onHeading(azimuth)

    override fun onAccuracy(type: Int, accuracy: Int) = checkCalibration(type, accuracy)

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        when (key) {
            DirectionPreferences.directionLatitude,
            DirectionPreferences.directionLongitude,
            DirectionPreferences.directionLabel,
            DirectionPreferences.useMapsTarget -> loadTarget()
            DirectionPreferences.directionGimbalLock -> sensor.gimbalLock = DirectionPreferences.isGimbalLock()
        }
    }

    private fun usesMapsTarget() = DirectionPreferences.isUsingMapsTarget() && GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet()

    private fun loadTarget() {
        val coordinates = if (usesMapsTarget()) GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates() else DirectionPreferences.getTargetCoordinates()
        target = LatLng(coordinates[0].toDouble(), coordinates[1].toDouble())
        state.target = if (usesMapsTarget()) getString(R.string.using_maps_target) else DirectionPreferences.getTargetLabel() ?: getString(R.string.not_available)
        val formatted = DMSConverter.getFormattedCoordinates(target, activity)
        state.coordinates = listOf(
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_latitude), formatted[0]),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_longitude), formatted[1]),
        )
        updateTarget()
    }

    private fun updateTarget() {
        val current = location ?: return
        state.bearing = LocationExtension.calculateBearingAngle(current.latitude, current.longitude, target.latitude, target.longitude).toFloat()
        state.displacement = DistanceText.format(activity, LocationExtension.measureDisplacement(arrayOf(LatLng(current.latitude, current.longitude), target)).toDouble(), MainPreferences.isMetric())
    }

}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/GPS.kt

package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.location.Location
import android.view.MotionEvent
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.core.content.getSystemService
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.IntOffset
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.callbacks.BottomBarHost
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.LocationDatabase
import app.simple.positional.decorations.maps.Maps
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.math.MovementThreshold
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toFeet
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toKiloMetersPerHour
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toMilesPerHour
import app.simple.positional.model.Locations
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.GpsActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.GpsScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.GpsState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MapRotation
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MessageDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PinCustomizationDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CoordinateInput
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CustomLocationForm
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.TargetCoordinatesDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CoordinateDetailsDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.LocationDetailsDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.LocationSummaryDialog
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationShare
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.toAnnotatedString
import app.simple.positional.util.DMSConverter
import app.simple.positional.util.Direction.getDirectionNameFromAzimuth
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationPrompt
import app.simple.positional.util.MapIntents
import app.simple.positional.util.NetworkCheck.isNetworkAvailable
import app.simple.positional.util.ReverseGeocoder
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import kotlinx.coroutines.Job
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class GPS(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), GpsActions, MapsCallbacks {
    private val state: GpsState
    private val targetForm = CustomLocationForm()
    private var accuracySeries by mutableStateOf(emptyList<Float>())
    private var altitudeSeries by mutableStateOf(emptyList<Float>())
    private val maps: Maps
    private val viewModel: LocationViewModel
    private val addressThreshold = MovementThreshold(ADDRESS_DISTANCE)
    private var addressJob: Job? = null
    private var location: Location? = null
    private var pressed: LatLng? = null
    private var accuracyRow = InfoRow("")
    private var altitudeRow = InfoRow("")
    private var latencyRow = InfoRow("")

    init {
        viewModel = ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java]
        state = GpsState(MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate())
        state.fullScreen = !isLandscapeOrientation && consumeFullScreenShortcut()
        readPreferences()
        accuracyRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_accuracy), PENDING)
        altitudeRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_altitude), PENDING)
        latencyRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_latency), PENDING)
        publishLocationRows()
        state.provider = providerRows(PENDING)
        state.movement = listOf(R.string.gps_bearing, R.string.gps_direction, R.string.gps_speed).map { InfoRow(getString(it), PENDING) }
        state.coordinates = listOf(R.string.gps_latitude, R.string.gps_longitude).map { InfoRow(getString(it), PENDING) }
        state.address = PENDING
    }

    init {
        maps = Maps(activity).apply {
            alpha = 0f
            onCreate(null)
            setOnMapsCallbackListener(this@GPS)
            onTouch = { event, _ ->
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> unregister()
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> registerWithRunnable()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        GpsScreen(state, maps, topInset, isLandscapeOrientation, this)
    }

    init {
        owner.lifecycle.addObserver(MapLifecycle(maps))
        if (state.fullScreen) bottomBar(visible = false)

        if (!LocationPrompt.checkGooglePlayServices(activity)) {
            val message = getString(R.string.play_services_error)
            dialogs.show { MessageDialog(message, dialogs::dismiss) }
        }

        if (state.custom) {
            val coordinates = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()
            showCoordinates(coordinates[0].toDouble(), coordinates[1].toDouble())
        }

        viewModel.getLocation().observe(owner, ::onLocation)
        viewModel.getProvider().observe(owner) {
            state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity)
            state.provider = providerRows(it)
        }
        viewModel.latency.observe(owner) {
            val unit = getString(if (it.second) R.string.seconds else R.string.milliseconds)
            latencyRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_latency), "${it.first} $unit", it.second && it.first.toDouble() > LATE)
            publishLocationRows()
        }
        viewModel.targetData.observe(owner) { state.target = it.toAnnotatedString() }
        viewModel.accuracyGraphData.observe(owner) { accuracySeries = it.toList() }
        viewModel.altitudeGraphData.observe(owner) { altitudeSeries = it.toList() }
    }

    override fun onCleared() {
        addressJob?.cancel()
        addressThreshold.reset()
    }


    private fun onLocation(location: Location) {
        this.location = location
        MainPreferences.setLastLatitude(location.latitude.toFloat())
        MainPreferences.setLastLongitude(location.longitude.toFloat())
        MainPreferences.setLastAltitude(location.altitude.toFloat())

        val metric = MainPreferences.isMetric()
        val length = getString(if (metric) R.string.meter else R.string.feet)
        val accuracy = if (metric) location.accuracy.toDouble() else location.accuracy.toDouble().toFeet()
        val altitude = if (metric) location.altitude else location.altitude.toFeet()
        val kmh = location.speed.toDouble().toKiloMetersPerHour()
        val speed = if (metric) kmh else kmh.toMilesPerHour()
        val direction = if (location.speed > 0f) getDirectionNameFromAzimuth(activity, location.bearing.toDouble()) else getString(R.string.not_available)

        state.fixed = true
        state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity)
        state.provider = providerRows(location.provider?.uppercase(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale()) ?: getString(R.string.not_available))
        accuracyRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_accuracy), "${round(accuracy, 2)} $length")
        altitudeRow = InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_altitude), "${round(altitude, 2)} $length")
        publishLocationRows()
        state.movement = listOf(
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_bearing), "${location.bearing}°"),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_direction), direction),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_speed), "${round(speed, 2)} ${getString(if (metric) R.string.kilometer_hour else R.string.miles_hour)}"),
        )
        state.speed = if (metric) location.speed.toKiloMetersPerHour() else location.speed.toMilesPerHour()

        if (!state.custom) {
            maps.setFirstLocation(location)
            maps.location = location
            maps.addMarker(LatLng(location.latitude, location.longitude))
            showCoordinates(location.latitude, location.longitude)
        }
    }

    private fun showCoordinates(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        state.coordinates = listOf(
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_latitude), DMSConverter.getFormattedLatitude(latitude, activity)),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_longitude), DMSConverter.getFormattedLongitude(longitude, activity)),
        )
        if (!addressThreshold.accept(latitude, longitude)) return
        addressJob?.cancel()
        addressJob = owner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            state.address = if (isNetworkAvailable(activity)) {
                ReverseGeocoder.addressLine(activity, latitude, longitude) ?: getString(R.string.no_address_found)
            } else {
                addressThreshold.reset()
                getString(R.string.internet_connection_alert)
            }
        }
    }

    private fun providerRows(source: String) = listOf(
        InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_status), getString(if (getLocationStatus(activity)) R.string.gps_enabled else R.string.gps_disabled)),
        InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_source), source),
    )

    private fun publishLocationRows() {
        state.location = listOf(accuracyRow, altitudeRow, latencyRow)
    }

    private fun readPreferences() {
        state.pin = LocationPins.getLocationPin()
        state.targetMode = GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode()
        state.targetSet = GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet()
        state.toolsLeft = GPSPreferences.isToolsGravityLeft()
        state.volumeKeys = GPSPreferences.isUsingVolumeKeys()
        state.rotation = when {
            GPSPreferences.isCompassRotation() -> MapRotation.Compass
            GPSPreferences.isBearingRotation() -> MapRotation.Bearing
            else -> MapRotation.North
        }
    }

    private fun consumeFullScreenShortcut(): Boolean {
        val intent = activity.intent ?: return false
        if (intent.action != FULL_SCREEN_ACTION) return false
        intent.action = null
        return true
    }

    private fun bottomBar(visible: Boolean) {
        (activity as? BottomBarHost)?.showBottomBar(visible)
    }

    override fun onMapInitialized() {
    }

    override fun onMapClicked() {
        if (isLandscapeOrientation) return
        state.fullScreen = !state.fullScreen
        bottomBar(!state.fullScreen)
    }

    override fun onMapLongClicked(latLng: LatLng) {
        val point = maps.screenPoint(latLng) ?: return
        pressed = latLng
        state.pressed = IntOffset(point.x, point.y)
    }

    override fun menu() = menuSheet.show {
        PanelMenus.gps(onPin = ::pinCustomization) {
            openPage(HtmlPage.MEDIA_KEYS)
        }
    }

    override fun customLocation() {
        val (latitude, longitude) = coordinates()
        dialogs.show { LocationSummaryDialog(latitude, longitude, state.custom, dialogs::dismiss) }
    }

    private fun coordinates(): Pair<Double, Double> {
        val current = location
        if (!state.custom && current != null) return current.latitude to current.longitude
        val stored = if (state.custom) MainPreferences.getCoordinates() else MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
        return stored[0].toDouble() to stored[1].toDouble()
    }

    override fun pinCustomization() = dialogs.show { PinCustomizationDialog(dialogs::dismiss) }

    override fun locate(close: Boolean) {
        if (getLocationStatus(activity)) {
            maps.isMapMovementEnabled = true
            maps.resetCamera(if (close) CLOSE_ZOOM else GPSPreferences.getMapZoom())
        } else {
            LocationPrompt.displayLocationSettingsRequest(activity)
        }
    }

    override fun toggleTargetMode() = GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerMode(!GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode())

    override fun confirmTarget() {
        GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerMode(false)
        maps.setTargetMarker(null)
    }

    override fun targetCoordinates() {
        val target = GPSPreferences.getTargetMarkerCoordinates()
        targetForm.fill("", CoordinateInput.format(target[0].toDouble()), CoordinateInput.format(target[1].toDouble()))
        dialogs.show {
            TargetCoordinatesDialog(
                targetForm,
                onSearch = {
                    search { result ->
                        targetForm.fill(result.address, CoordinateInput.format(result.latitude), CoordinateInput.format(result.longitude))
                    }
                },
                onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss,
            ) { latitude, longitude ->
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLatitude(latitude.toFloat())
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerLongitude(longitude.toFloat())
                GPSPreferences.setTargetMarker(true)
                dialogs.dismiss()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun removeTarget() = GPSPreferences.setTargetMarker(false)

    override fun toggleToolsSide() = GPSPreferences.setToolsGravity(!GPSPreferences.isToolsGravityLeft())

    override fun rotate(rotation: MapRotation) {
        GPSPreferences.setCompassRotation(rotation == MapRotation.Compass)
        GPSPreferences.setBearingRotation(rotation == MapRotation.Bearing)
        GPSPreferences.setNorthOnly(rotation == MapRotation.North)
    }

    override fun sheet(open: Boolean) {
        state.sheet = open
        if (open) maps.unregister() else maps.registerWithRunnable()
    }

    override fun save() {
        val current = location
        if (current == null) {
            state.message = R.string.location_not_saved
            return
        }
        val entry = Locations().apply {
            latitude = current.latitude
            longitude = current.longitude
            address = state.address
            date = System.currentTimeMillis()
        }
        val dao = LocationDatabase.getInstance(activity).locationDao()
        owner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            withContext(NonCancellable) { dao.insertLocation(entry) }
            state.message = R.string.location_saved
        }
    }

    override fun copy() {
        val clipboard = activity.getSystemService<ClipboardManager>() ?: return
        val text = buildString {
            section(getString(R.string.gps_provider), state.provider)
            section(getString(R.string.gps_location), state.location)
            section(getString(R.string.gps_movement), state.movement)
            if (state.custom) append(getString(R.string.specified_location_notice)).append("\n\n")
            section(getString(R.string.gps_coordinates), state.coordinates + InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_address) + ":", state.address))
        }.trim()
        clipboard.setPrimaryClip(ClipData.newPlainText(getString(R.string.gps_info), text))
        state.message = R.string.info_copied
    }

    private fun StringBuilder.section(title: String, rows: List<InfoRow>) {
        append(title).append('\n')
        rows.forEach { append(it.label).append(' ').append(it.value).append('\n') }
        append('\n')
    }

    override fun share() {
        val (latitude, longitude) = coordinates()
        LocationShare.share(activity, latitude, longitude, state.address)
    }

    override fun locationDetails() = dialogs.show {
        LocationDetailsDialog(state.location, accuracySeries, altitudeSeries, MainPreferences.isMetric(), dialogs::dismiss)
    }

    override fun coordinateDetails() {
        val (latitude, longitude) = coordinates()
        dialogs.show { CoordinateDetailsDialog(latitude, longitude, dialogs::dismiss) }
    }

    override fun showTarget() {
        sheet(false)
        if (GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerSet()) maps.moveCameraToTarget() else GPSPreferences.setTargetMarkerMode(true)
    }

    override fun setPressedTarget() {
        pressed?.let(maps::setTargetMarker)
        state.pressed = null
    }

    override fun navigatePressed() {
        state.pressed = null
        val latLng = pressed ?: return
        MapIntents.navigate(activity, latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude)
    }

    override fun zoom(zoomIn: Boolean) = if (zoomIn) maps.zoomIn() else maps.zoomOut()

    override fun recenter() = maps.resetCamera(GPSPreferences.getMapZoom())

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        if (key in gpsKeys) readPreferences()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val PENDING = "-"
        private const val FULL_SCREEN_ACTION = "action_map_panel_full"
        private const val CLOSE_ZOOM = 18F
        private const val LATE = 5.0
        private const val ADDRESS_DISTANCE = 25.0
        private val gpsKeys = setOf(
            GPSPreferences.pinSkin,
            GPSPreferences.toolsGravity,
            GPSPreferences.isTargetMarkerMode,
            GPSPreferences.mapTargetMarker,
            GPSPreferences.compassRotation,
            GPSPreferences.useBearingRotation,
            GPSPreferences.isNorthOnly,
            GPSPreferences.useVolumeKeys,
        )
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Level.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.SensorPanel
import app.simple.positional.math.Tilt
import app.simple.positional.preferences.LevelPreferences
import app.simple.positional.sensors.GravitySensor
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.LevelScreen

class Level(activity: ComponentActivity) : SensorPanel(activity) {
    private var tilt by mutableStateOf(Tilt.zero)
    private var square by mutableStateOf(false)
    private val sensor: GravitySensor

    init {
        sensor = GravitySensor(activity) { tilt = it }
        lifecycle.addObserver(sensor)
        square = LevelPreferences.isSquareStyle()
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        LevelScreen({ tilt }, square, topInset) { LevelPreferences.setSquareStyle(!square) }
    }

    init {
        if (!sensor.isAvailable) showSensorError()
    }

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        if (key == LevelPreferences.isSquareStyle) square = LevelPreferences.isSquareStyle()
    }

}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Measure.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.location.Location
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.callbacks.BottomBarHost
import app.simple.positional.decorations.measure.MeasureMaps
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.extensions.maps.MapsCallbacks
import app.simple.positional.math.GeoDistance
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MeasureState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.NameNoteDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.util.DistanceText
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationExtension.getLocationStatus
import app.simple.positional.util.LocationPrompt
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.MeasureViewModel
import com.google.android.gms.maps.model.LatLng
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure as MeasureModel

class Measure(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), MeasureActions, MapsCallbacks {
    private val state = MeasureState()
    private val locationViewModel: LocationViewModel
    private val measureViewModel: MeasureViewModel
    private val maps: MeasureMaps
    private var location: Location? = null

    init {
        locationViewModel = ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java]
        measureViewModel = ViewModelProvider(activity)[MeasureViewModel::class.java]
        readPreferences()
    }

    init {
        maps = MeasureMaps(activity).apply {
            alpha = 0f
            onCreate(null)
            setOnMapsCallbackListener(this@Measure)
        }
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        MeasureScreen(state, maps, topInset, this)
    }

    init {
        owner.lifecycle.addObserver(MapLifecycle(maps))
        if (state.fullScreen) bottomBar(visible = false)
        locationViewModel.getProvider().observe(owner) { state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity) }
    }


    override fun onMapInitialized() {
        locationViewModel.getLocation().observe(owner) {
            location = it
            state.fixed = true
            state.enabled = getLocationStatus(activity)
            val latLng = LatLng(it.latitude, it.longitude)
            maps.setFirstLocation(latLng)
            maps.location = it
            maps.addMarker(latLng)
        }
        measureViewModel.getMeasure().observe(owner) { measure ->
            if (MeasurePreferences.isMeasureSelected() && measure != null) {
                maps.createMeasurePolylines(measure)
                state.selected = true
                state.name = measure.name.orEmpty()
                showSummary(measure)
                state.current = ""
            } else {
                clearMeasure()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onMapClicked() {
        if (isLandscapeOrientation) return
        state.fullScreen = !state.fullScreen
        bottomBar(!state.fullScreen)
    }

    override fun onLineAdded(measurePoint: MeasurePoint) {
        measureViewModel.addMeasurePoint(measurePoint) { measure -> activity.runOnUiThread { showSummary(measure) } }
    }

    override fun onLineDeleted(measurePoint: MeasurePoint?) {
        measureViewModel.removeMeasurePoint(measurePoint) { measure -> activity.runOnUiThread { showSummary(measure) } }
    }

    override fun onCameraDistance(latLng: LatLng?) {
        val last = maps.getMeasurePoints().lastOrNull()
        state.current = if (latLng == null || last == null) "" else {
            distance(GeoDistance.meters(last.latitude, last.longitude, latLng.latitude, latLng.longitude))
        }
    }

    private fun showSummary(measure: MeasureModel) {
        val points = measure.measurePoints
        state.hasPoints = points.isNotEmpty()
        state.summary = listOf(
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.total), points.size.toString()),
            InfoRow(getString(R.string.gps_displacement), distance(GeoDistance.path(points.map { it.latitude to it.longitude }))),
        )
    }

    private fun distance(meters: Double) = DistanceText.format(activity, meters, MainPreferences.isMetric())

    private fun clearMeasure() {
        maps.clear()
        state.selected = false
        state.hasPoints = false
        state.current = ""
    }

    private fun readPreferences() {
        state.toolsLeft = MeasurePreferences.isToolsGravityToLeft()
        state.compass = MeasurePreferences.isCompassRotation()
        state.wrapped = MeasurePreferences.arePolylinesWrapped()
    }

    private fun bottomBar(visible: Boolean) {
        (activity as? BottomBarHost)?.showBottomBar(visible)
    }

    override fun addMeasure() {
        val title = getString(R.string.create_new)
        dialogs.show {
            NameNoteDialog(title, isValid = { it.isNotEmpty() }, onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss) { name, note ->
                measureViewModel.addMeasure(name, note)
                dialogs.dismiss()
            }
        }
    }

    override fun measures() = navigate(Routes.MEASURES)

    override fun menu() = menuSheet.show { PanelMenus.measure() }

    override fun locate(close: Boolean) {
        if (!getLocationStatus(activity)) {
            LocationPrompt.displayLocationSettingsRequest(activity)
            return
        }
        val current = location ?: return
        if (close) {
            maps.resetCamera(CLOSE_ZOOM)
        } else {
            maps.moveMapCamera(
                LatLng(current.latitude, current.longitude),
                MeasurePreferences.getMapZoom(),
                MeasurePreferences.getMapTilt(),
                MeasurePreferences.getMapBearing(),
            )
        }
    }

    override fun addPoint() = maps.addPolyline()

    override fun removeLast() = maps.removeRecentPolyline()

    override fun wrap() = MeasurePreferences.invertPolylinesWrapped()

    override fun toggleCompass() = MeasurePreferences.invertCompassRotation()

    override fun toggleToolsSide() = MeasurePreferences.invertToolsGravity()

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        when (key) {
            MeasurePreferences.LAST_SELECTED_MEASURE -> if (!MeasurePreferences.isMeasureSelected()) clearMeasure()
            MeasurePreferences.TOOLS_GRAVITY, MeasurePreferences.COMPASS_ROTATION, MeasurePreferences.POLYLINES_WRAPPED -> readPreferences()
        }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val CLOSE_ZOOM = 18F
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Settings.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import android.Manifest
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.Settings as AndroidSettings
import android.view.WindowManager
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.core.net.toUri
import app.simple.positional.BuildConfig
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsActions
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsLabels
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.SettingsState
import app.simple.positional.util.AppIcons
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils

class Settings(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity), SettingsActions {
    private var state by mutableStateOf(SettingsState())
    private var appSettingsOpened = false
    private val permissionLauncher = register(ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()) { result ->
        if (result.values.any { !it } && !appSettingsOpened) {
            appSettingsOpened = true
            startActivity(Intent(AndroidSettings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS).apply {
                data = Uri.fromParts("package", activity.packageName, null)
            })
        }
        refresh()
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        SettingsScreen(state, this, topInset)
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        appSettingsOpened = false
        refresh()
    }

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        if (key == MainPreferences.accentColor) {
            activity.recreate()
        } else {
            refresh()
        }
    }

    private fun refresh() {
        state = SettingsState(
            hasLocationPermission = PermissionUtils.checkPermission(activity),
            themeMode = if (MainPreferences.isDayNightOn()) SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO else MainPreferences.getTheme(),
            isMetric = MainPreferences.isMetric(),
            coordinatesFormat = MainPreferences.getCoordinatesFormat(),
            locationProvider = MainPreferences.getLocationProvider(),
            language = MainPreferences.getAppLanguage() ?: SettingsLabels.LANGUAGE_DEFAULT,
            isCustomLocation = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate(),
            keepScreenOn = MainPreferences.isScreenOn(),
            skipSplashScreen = MainPreferences.getSkipSplashScreen(),
            iconIndex = AppIcons.current(activity),
            cornerRadius = MainPreferences.getCornerRadius() * 5,
            versionName = BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME,
        )
    }

    override fun requestPermission() {
        permissionLauncher.launch(arrayOf(Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION))
    }

    override fun setThemeMode(mode: Int) {
        MainPreferences.setDayNight(mode == SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO)
        if (mode != SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO) MainPreferences.setTheme(mode)
        activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun openAccent() = navigate(Routes.ACCENT)

    override fun setIcon(index: Int) {
        AppIcons.select(activity, index)
        refresh()
    }

    override fun setCornerRadius(value: Int) {
        if (value == MainPreferences.getCornerRadius() * 5) return
        MainPreferences.setCornerRadius(value)
        activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun setSkipSplashScreen(enabled: Boolean) = MainPreferences.setSkipSplashScreen(enabled)

    override fun openArts() = navigate(Routes.ARTS)

    override fun setMetric(metric: Boolean) = MainPreferences.setUnit(metric)

    override fun setCoordinatesFormat(format: Int) = MainPreferences.setCoordinatesFormat(format)

    override fun setLocationProvider(provider: String) = MainPreferences.setLocationProvider(provider)

    override fun openCustomLocations() = navigate(Routes.CUSTOM_LOCATIONS)

    override fun setCustomLocation(enabled: Boolean) {
        if (enabled) openCustomLocations() else MainPreferences.setCustomCoordinates(false)
    }

    override fun setKeepScreenOn(enabled: Boolean) {
        MainPreferences.setScreenOn(enabled)
        if (enabled) {
            activity.window.addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        } else {
            activity.window.clearFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_KEEP_SCREEN_ON)
        }
    }

    override fun setLanguage(code: String) {
        if (code == MainPreferences.getAppLanguage()) return
        MainPreferences.setAppLanguage(code)
        activity.recreate()
    }

    override fun openLegal(page: Int) {
        HtmlPage.forTitle(page)?.let(::openPage)
    }

    override fun openReleases() = openUrl("$REPOSITORY_URL/releases")

    override fun openRepository() = openUrl(REPOSITORY_URL)

    override fun openIssues() = openUrl("$REPOSITORY_URL/issues")

    private fun openUrl(url: String) {
        runCatching { startActivity(Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, url.toUri())) }
    }

    companion object {
        private const val REPOSITORY_URL = "https://github.com/kusl/hamza417-positional"
    }
}

################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.panels

import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.Dp
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.os.SystemClock
import androidx.lifecycle.Lifecycle
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.lifecycleScope
import androidx.lifecycle.repeatOnLifecycle
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.ui.navigation.Routes
import app.simple.positional.constants.ClockSkinsConstants.clockNeedleSkins
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.extensions.panel.PanelController
import app.simple.positional.math.ClockMotion
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockScreen
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockState
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.Choice
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ChoiceDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.ClockNeedleDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.LocationSummaryDialog
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.PanelMenus
import app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel.LocationViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.time.ZoneId

class Time(activity: ComponentActivity) : PanelController(activity) {
    private val state = ClockState()
    private val report: ClockReport
    private var latitude = 0.0
    private var longitude = 0.0
    private var altitude = 0.0
    private var lastSky = 0L

    init {
        report = ClockReport(activity)
        applyPreferences()
    }

    @Composable
    override fun Content(topInset: Dp) {
        ClockScreen(
            state,
            topInset,
            onTimezone = { navigate(Routes.TIMEZONE) },
            onMenu = ::showMenu,
            onLocation = { dialogs.show { LocationSummaryDialog(latitude, longitude, state.customLocation, dialogs::dismiss) } },
        )
    }

    private fun showMenu() = menuSheet.show {
        PanelMenus.clock(
            onNeedles = {
                dialogs.show { ClockNeedleDialog(ClockPreferences.getClockNeedleTheme(), dialogs::dismiss) { ClockPreferences.setClockNeedleTheme(it) } }
            },
            onMotionType = ::showMotionTypes,
        )
    }

    private fun showMotionTypes() {
        val title = getString(R.string.motion_type)
        val choices = motionTypes.map { (value, label) -> Choice(value, getString(label)) }
        dialogs.show {
            ChoiceDialog(title, choices, ClockPreferences.getMovementType(), onDismiss = dialogs::dismiss) {
                ClockPreferences.setMovementType(it)
                dialogs.dismiss()
            }
        }
    }

    init {
        ViewModelProvider(activity)[LocationViewModel::class.java].getLocation().observe(owner) {
            if (state.customLocation) return@observe
            latitude = it.latitude
            longitude = it.longitude
            altitude = it.altitude
            if (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - lastSky > SKY_MIN_INTERVAL) refreshSkyAsync()
        }
        owner.lifecycleScope.launch {
            owner.repeatOnLifecycle(Lifecycle.State.RESUMED) {
                launch {
                    while (true) {
                        val zone = state.zone
                        state.time = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { report.time(zone) }
                        delay(1000L - System.currentTimeMillis() % 1000L)
                    }
                }
                while (true) {
                    refreshSky()
                    delay(SKY_INTERVAL)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private suspend fun refreshSky() {
        lastSky = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
        val zone = state.zone
        state.sky = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { report.sky(latitude, longitude, altitude, zone) }
    }

    private fun refreshSkyAsync() {
        owner.lifecycleScope.launch { refreshSky() }
    }

    private fun applyPreferences() {
        state.zone = runCatching { ZoneId.of(ClockPreferences.getTimeZone()) }.getOrDefault(ZoneId.systemDefault())
        state.motion = ClockMotion.of(ClockPreferences.getMovementType())
        state.face24 = ClockPreferences.isClockFace24Hour()
        state.needles = clockNeedleSkins.getOrElse(ClockPreferences.getClockNeedleTheme()) { clockNeedleSkins[0] }.toList()
        state.pin = LocationPins.locationsPins.getOrElse(GPSPreferences.getPinSkin()) { R.drawable.ic_pin_01 }
        state.customLocation = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()
        if (state.customLocation) {
            latitude = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()[0].toDouble()
            longitude = MainPreferences.getCoordinates()[1].toDouble()
            altitude = 0.0
        } else {
            latitude = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()[0].toDouble()
            longitude = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()[1].toDouble()
            altitude = MainPreferences.getLastAltitude().toDouble()
        }
    }

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        if (key !in clockKeys) return
        applyPreferences()
        refreshSkyAsync()
    }

    companion object {
        private const val SKY_INTERVAL = 60_000L
        private val motionTypes = listOf(
            "smooth" to R.string.motion_type_smooth,
            "tick" to R.string.motion_type_tick,
            "oscillate" to R.string.tick_oscillate,
            "tick_smooth" to R.string.tick_smooth,
            "mechanical" to R.string.mechanical,
        )
        private const val SKY_MIN_INTERVAL = 10_000L
        private val clockKeys = setOf(
            ClockPreferences.clockNeedleMovementType,
            ClockPreferences.clockNeedle,
            ClockPreferences.timezone,
            ClockPreferences.is24HourFace,
            MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate,
            MainPreferences.latitude,
            MainPreferences.longitude,
            MainPreferences.unit,
            GPSPreferences.pinSkin,
        )
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/panels/Trail.kt
