# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/MapSearchViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.location.Address
import android.location.Geocoder
import android.os.Build
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CoordinateInput
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.CustomLocationForm
import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.MapSearchQuery
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.FlowPreview
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.debounce
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.distinctUntilChanged
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.mapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlin.coroutines.resume

@OptIn(FlowPreview::class, ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class MapSearchViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    val form = CustomLocationForm()
    private val query = MutableStateFlow("")

    val suggestions: StateFlow<List<Address>> = query
        .debounce { if (it.isEmpty()) 0L else MapSearchQuery.DELAY }
        .map(MapSearchQuery::normalize)
        .distinctUntilChanged()
        .mapLatest { text -> text?.let { search(it) }.orEmpty().filter { it.hasLatitude() && it.hasLongitude() } }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(5_000), emptyList())

    init {
        val last = MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
        form.fill("", CoordinateInput.format(last[0].toDouble()), CoordinateInput.format(last[1].toDouble()))
    }

    fun onAddressChange(text: String) {
        form.address = text
        query.value = text
    }

    fun onCameraIdle(latitude: Double, longitude: Double) {
        form.latitude = CoordinateInput.format(latitude)
        form.longitude = CoordinateInput.format(longitude)
    }

    fun pick(address: Address) {
        query.value = ""
        form.fill(address.getAddressLine(0) ?: form.address, CoordinateInput.format(address.latitude), CoordinateInput.format(address.longitude))
    }

    private suspend fun search(text: String): List<Address> {
        if (!Geocoder.isPresent()) return emptyList()
        val geocoder = Geocoder(getApplication())
        return if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            geocoder.searchAsync(text)
        } else {
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                runCatching { geocoder.getFromLocationName(text, MapSearchQuery.MAX_RESULTS) }.getOrNull().orEmpty()
            }
        }
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU)
    private suspend fun Geocoder.searchAsync(text: String): List<Address> = suspendCancellableCoroutine { continuation ->
        getFromLocationName(text, MapSearchQuery.MAX_RESULTS, object : Geocoder.GeocodeListener {
            override fun onGeocode(addresses: MutableList<Address>) {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(addresses)
            }

            override fun onError(errorMessage: String?) {
                if (continuation.isActive) continuation.resume(emptyList())
            }
        })
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/MeasureViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import androidx.lifecycle.LiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.MutableLiveData
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.MeasureDatabase
import app.simple.positional.extensions.viewmodel.WrappedViewModel
import app.simple.positional.model.Measure
import app.simple.positional.model.MeasurePoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MeasurePreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class MeasureViewModel(application: Application) : WrappedViewModel(application) {
    private val dao = MeasureDatabase.getInstance(application).measureDao()

    val measures: StateFlow<List<Measure>?> = dao.observeAll()
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    private val measure: MutableLiveData<Measure> by lazy {
        MutableLiveData<Measure>().also {
            loadMeasure(MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure())
        }
    }

    fun getMeasure(): LiveData<Measure> {
        return measure
    }

    private fun loadMeasure(id: Long) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            measure.postValue(dao.getMeasureById(id))
        }
    }

    fun addMeasure(name: String, note: String) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val created = Measure(name, note)
            withContext(NonCancellable) {
                dao.insertMeasure(created)
                MeasurePreferences.setLastSelectedMeasure(created.dateCreated)
            }
            loadMeasure(created.dateCreated)
        }
    }

    fun addMeasurePoint(measurePoint: MeasurePoint, onAdded: (Measure) -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            measure.value?.let {
                it.measurePoints.add(measurePoint)
                dao.updateMeasure(it)
                onAdded(it)
            }
        }
    }

    fun removeMeasurePoint(measurePoint: MeasurePoint?, onRemoved: (Measure) -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            val current = measure.value
            if (measurePoint != null && current != null) {
                current.measurePoints.remove(measurePoint)
                dao.updateMeasure(current)
                onRemoved(current)
            }
        }
    }

    fun deleteMeasure(measure: Measure) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
            withContext(NonCancellable) {
                dao.deleteMeasure(measure)
                if (MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure() == measure.dateCreated) MeasurePreferences.setLastSelectedMeasure(-1)
            }
        }
    }

    override fun onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences: SharedPreferences?, key: String?) {
        super.onSharedPreferenceChanged(sharedPreferences, key)
        when (key) {
            MeasurePreferences.LAST_SELECTED_MEASURE -> {
                loadMeasure(MeasurePreferences.getLastSelectedMeasure())
            }
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/TrailDataViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailPointDatabase
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailPoint
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.ExperimentalCoroutinesApi
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flatMapLatest
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.flowOf
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

@OptIn(ExperimentalCoroutinesApi::class)
class TrailDataViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val name = MutableStateFlow(TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail())
    val trail: StateFlow<String> = name.asStateFlow()

    val points: StateFlow<List<TrailPoint>> = name
        .flatMapLatest { if (it.isEmpty()) flowOf(emptyList()) else dao(it).observeAll() }
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), emptyList())

    fun select(trail: String) {
        name.value = trail
    }

    fun save(point: TrailPoint) = persist { dao(it).insertTrailData(point) }

    fun delete(point: TrailPoint?) {
        point ?: return
        persist { dao(it).deleteTrailData(point) }
    }

    private fun dao(trail: String) = TrailPointDatabase.getInstance(getApplication(), trail).trailDataDao()

    private fun persist(block: suspend (String) -> Unit) {
        val trail = name.value
        if (trail.isEmpty()) return
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { withContext(NonCancellable) { block(trail) } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/viewmodels/viewmodel/TrailsViewModel.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.viewmodels.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailDatabase
import app.simple.positional.database.instances.TrailPointDatabase
import app.simple.positional.model.TrailEntry
import app.simple.positional.preferences.TrailPreferences
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.NonCancellable
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.SharingStarted
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.stateIn
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class TrailsViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val dao = TrailDatabase.getInstance(application).trailDao()

    val trails: StateFlow<List<TrailEntry>?> = dao.observeAll()
        .stateIn(viewModelScope, SharingStarted.WhileSubscribed(STOP_TIMEOUT), null)

    fun addTrail(trailEntry: TrailEntry) = persist {
        dao.insertTrail(trailEntry)
        TrailPreferences.setCurrentTrailName(trailEntry.trailName)
    }

    fun deleteTrail(trailEntry: TrailEntry) = persist {
        dao.deleteTrail(trailEntry)
        if (TrailPreferences.getCurrentTrail() == trailEntry.trailName) TrailPreferences.setCurrentTrailName("")
        TrailPointDatabase.close(trailEntry.trailName)
        getApplication<Application>().deleteDatabase("${trailEntry.trailName}.db")
    }

    private fun persist(block: suspend () -> Unit) {
        viewModelScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { withContext(NonCancellable) { block() } }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val STOP_TIMEOUT = 5_000L
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/ClockWidget2x2.java
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.widgets;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.SystemClock;
import app.simple.positional.services.ClockWidgetService2x2;
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils;

public class ClockWidget2x2 extends AppWidgetProvider {
    @Override
    public void onUpdate(Context context, AppWidgetManager appWidgetManager, int[] appWidgetIds) {
        super.onUpdate(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetIds);
        startService(context);
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        alarmManager.setRepeating(AlarmManager.ELAPSED_REALTIME, SystemClock.elapsedRealtime(), 60000, getService(context));
    }

    @Override
    public void onEnabled(Context context) {
        super.onEnabled(context);
        startService(context);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisabled(Context context) {
        super.onDisabled(context);
        cancelWidgetProcess(context);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDeleted(Context context, int[] appWidgetIds) {
        super.onDeleted(context, appWidgetIds);
        cancelWidgetProcess(context);
    }

    private void cancelWidgetProcess(Context context) {
        try {
            context.stopService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService2x2.class));
        } catch (IllegalStateException ignored) {
        }
        ((AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE)).cancel(getService(context));
    }

    private void startService(Context context) {
        if (PermissionUtils.INSTANCE.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context)) {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                    try {
                        context.startForegroundService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService2x2.class));
                    } catch (ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException e) {
                        e.printStackTrace();
                    }
                } else {
                    context.startForegroundService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService2x2.class));
                }
            } else {
                context.startService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService2x2.class));
            }
        }
    }

    private PendingIntent getService(Context context) {
        return PendingIntent.getService(context,
                1522,
                new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService2x2.class),
                PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE | PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT);
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/ClockWidget4x4.java
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.widgets;

import android.app.AlarmManager;
import android.app.ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager;
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider;
import android.content.Context;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.SystemClock;
import app.simple.positional.services.ClockWidgetService4x4;
import app.simple.positional.util.PermissionUtils;

public class ClockWidget4x4 extends AppWidgetProvider {
    @Override
    public void onUpdate(Context context, AppWidgetManager appWidgetManager, int[] appWidgetIds) {
        super.onUpdate(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetIds);
        startService(context);
        AlarmManager alarmManager = (AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE);
        alarmManager.setRepeating(AlarmManager.ELAPSED_REALTIME, SystemClock.elapsedRealtime(), 60000, getService(context));
    }

    @Override
    public void onEnabled(Context context) {
        super.onEnabled(context);
        startService(context);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisabled(Context context) {
        super.onDisabled(context);
        cancelWidgetProcess(context);
    }

    @Override
    public void onDeleted(Context context, int[] appWidgetIds) {
        super.onDeleted(context, appWidgetIds);
        cancelWidgetProcess(context);
    }

    private void cancelWidgetProcess(Context context) {
        try {
            context.stopService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService4x4.class));
        } catch (IllegalStateException ignored) {
        }
        ((AlarmManager) context.getSystemService(Context.ALARM_SERVICE)).cancel(getService(context));
    }

    private void startService(Context context) {
        if (PermissionUtils.INSTANCE.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context)) {
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                    try {
                        context.startForegroundService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService4x4.class));
                    } catch (ForegroundServiceStartNotAllowedException e) {
                        e.printStackTrace();
                    }
                } else {
                    context.startForegroundService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService4x4.class));
                }
            } else {
                context.startService(new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService4x4.class));
            }
        }
    }

    private PendingIntent getService(Context context) {
        return PendingIntent.getService(context,
                1544,
                new Intent(context, ClockWidgetService4x4.class),
                PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE | PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT);
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/MoonPhaseWidget.kt

package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.content.Context
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.glance.GlanceId
import androidx.glance.GlanceModifier
import androidx.glance.Image
import androidx.glance.ImageProvider
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidget
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidgetReceiver
import androidx.glance.appwidget.provideContent
import androidx.glance.background
import androidx.glance.layout.Alignment
import androidx.glance.layout.Column
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.glance.layout.padding
import androidx.glance.layout.size
import androidx.glance.text.Text
import androidx.glance.text.TextStyle
import androidx.glance.unit.ColorProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions
import app.simple.positional.util.MoonAngle
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.MoonIllumination
import java.time.Instant
import java.util.TimeZone

class MoonPhaseWidget : GlanceAppWidgetReceiver() {
    override val glanceAppWidget: GlanceAppWidget = MoonPhaseGlance()
}

class MoonPhaseGlance : GlanceAppWidget() {
    override suspend fun provideGlance(context: Context, id: GlanceId) {
        val phase = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            MoonIllumination.compute().timezone(TimeZone.getDefault().id).on(Instant.now()).execute().phase
        }
        val art = MoonAngle.getMoonPhaseGraphics(MathExtensions.round(phase, 2))
        val label = MoonAngle.getMoonPhase(context, phase)
        provideContent {
            Column(
                GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize().background(ImageProvider(R.drawable.bg_widget)).padding(20.dp),
                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Image(ImageProvider(art), contentDescription = label, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(120.dp))
                Text(label, style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textSecondary)))
            }
        }
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/SunTimeWidget.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.content.Context
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import androidx.glance.GlanceId
import androidx.glance.GlanceModifier
import androidx.glance.Image
import androidx.glance.ImageProvider
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidget
import androidx.glance.appwidget.GlanceAppWidgetReceiver
import androidx.glance.appwidget.provideContent
import androidx.glance.background
import androidx.glance.layout.Alignment
import androidx.glance.layout.Column
import androidx.glance.layout.Row
import androidx.glance.layout.defaultWeight
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.glance.layout.fillMaxWidth
import androidx.glance.layout.padding
import androidx.glance.layout.size
import androidx.glance.text.Text
import androidx.glance.text.TextStyle
import androidx.glance.unit.ColorProvider
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.TimeZone

class SunTimeWidget : GlanceAppWidgetReceiver() {
    override val glanceAppWidget: GlanceAppWidget = SunTimeGlance()
}

class SunTimeGlance : GlanceAppWidget() {
    override suspend fun provideGlance(context: Context, id: GlanceId) {
        SharedPreferences.init(context)
        val pin = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) R.drawable.ic_place_custom else LocationPins.getLocationPin()
        val pattern = if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) {
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("hh:mm a").withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
        } else {
            DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm")
        }
        val times = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val coordinates = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) MainPreferences.getCoordinates() else MainPreferences.getLastCoordinates()
            val sun = SunTimes.compute()
                .timezone(TimeZone.getDefault().id)
                .on(Instant.now())
                .latitude(coordinates[0].toDouble())
                .longitude(coordinates[1].toDouble())
                .execute()
            listOf(
                R.drawable.ic_widget_sunrise to sun.rise,
                R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_noon to sun.noon,
                R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_set to sun.set,
                R.drawable.ic_widget_sun_nadir to sun.nadir,
            ).map { (icon, time) -> icon to (time?.let(pattern::format) ?: "--") }
        }
        val updated = context.getString(R.string.last_updated) + " • " + pattern.format(ZonedDateTime.now())
        provideContent {
            Column(GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize().background(ImageProvider(R.drawable.bg_widget)).padding(10.dp)) {
                Row(GlanceModifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(10.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Image(ImageProvider(pin), contentDescription = null, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(20.dp))
                    Text(
                        updated,
                        style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textTertiary)),
                        modifier = GlanceModifier.defaultWeight().padding(horizontal = 8.dp),
                    )
                    Text(context.getString(R.string.app_name), style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.dividerColor), fontSize = 12.sp))
                }
                Row(GlanceModifier.fillMaxSize(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    times.forEach { (icon, time) ->
                        Column(GlanceModifier.defaultWeight(), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                            Image(ImageProvider(icon), contentDescription = null, modifier = GlanceModifier.size(36.dp))
                            Text(time, style = TextStyle(color = ColorProvider(R.color.textTertiary), fontSize = 12.sp))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/SunTimeWidgetArt.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.widget.RemoteViews
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.constants.LauncherBackground
import app.simple.positional.constants.LocationPins
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.GPSPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences.getSharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.*

class SunTimeWidgetArt : AppWidgetProvider() {
    override fun onUpdate(context: Context, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager, appWidgetIds: IntArray) {
        onWidgetUpdate(context)

        super.onUpdate(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetIds)
    }

    override fun onEnabled(context: Context) {
        super.onEnabled(context)

        onWidgetUpdate(context)
    }

    private fun onWidgetUpdate(context: Context) {
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            SharedPreferences.init(context)

            val latitude = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) {
                getSharedPreferences().getFloat(MainPreferences.latitude, 0.0F).toDouble()
            } else {
                getSharedPreferences().getFloat(MainPreferences.lastLatitude, 0.0F).toDouble()
            }

            val longitude = if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) {
                getSharedPreferences().getFloat(MainPreferences.longitude, 0.0F).toDouble()
            } else {
                getSharedPreferences().getFloat(MainPreferences.lastLongitude, 0.0F).toDouble()
            }

            val pattern: DateTimeFormatter = if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) {
                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("hh:mm a").withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
            } else {
                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm")
            }

            val sunTimes = SunTimes.compute().timezone(Calendar.getInstance().timeZone.id).on(Instant.now()).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude).execute()
            val sunRise = pattern.format(sunTimes.rise)
            val sunNoon = pattern.format(sunTimes.noon)
            val sunSet = pattern.format(sunTimes.set)
            val sunNadir = pattern.format(sunTimes.nadir)

            val updateTime = context.getString(R.string.last_updated) + " • " + pattern.format(ZonedDateTime.now())

            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                val views = RemoteViews(context.packageName, R.layout.widget_suntime_art)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_sunrise, sunRise)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_sun_noon, sunNoon)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_sunset, sunSet)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_sun_nadir, sunNadir)

                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_suntime_heading, updateTime)

                views.setImageViewResource(R.id.widget_suntime_art,
                        LauncherBackground.vectorBackground[LauncherBackground.vectorBackground.indices.random()])

                if (MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()) {
                    views.setImageViewResource(R.id.widget_suntime_icon, R.drawable.ic_place_custom)
                } else {
                    views.setImageViewResource(R.id.widget_suntime_icon, LocationPins.locationsPins[GPSPreferences.getPinSkin()])
                }

                val componentName = ComponentName(context, SunTimeWidgetArt::class.java)
                val manager = AppWidgetManager.getInstance(context)
                manager.updateAppWidget(componentName, views)
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/main/java/app/simple/positional/widgets/TwilightWidget.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.widgets

import android.appwidget.AppWidgetManager
import android.appwidget.AppWidgetProvider
import android.content.ComponentName
import android.content.Context
import android.widget.RemoteViews
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.preferences.ClockPreferences
import app.simple.positional.preferences.MainPreferences
import app.simple.positional.singleton.SharedPreferences
import app.simple.positional.util.HtmlHelper
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.shredzone.commons.suncalc.SunTimes
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter

class TwilightWidget : AppWidgetProvider() {
    override fun onUpdate(context: Context?, appWidgetManager: AppWidgetManager?, appWidgetIds: IntArray?) {
        context?.updateWidget()
        super.onUpdate(context, appWidgetManager, appWidgetIds)
    }

    override fun onEnabled(context: Context?) {
        super.onEnabled(context)
        context?.updateWidget()
    }

    private fun Context.updateWidget() {
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default).launch {
            SharedPreferences.init(this@updateWidget)
            val preferences = SharedPreferences.getSharedPreferences()
            val custom = MainPreferences.isCustomCoordinate()
            val latitude = preferences.getFloat(if (custom) MainPreferences.latitude else MainPreferences.lastLatitude, 0f).toDouble()
            val longitude = preferences.getFloat(if (custom) MainPreferences.longitude else MainPreferences.lastLongitude, 0f).toDouble()

            val pattern: DateTimeFormatter = if (ClockPreferences.getDefaultClockTimeFormat()) {
                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("hh:mm a").withLocale(LocaleHelper.getAppLocale())
            } else {
                DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm")
            }
            val notAvailable = getString(R.string.not_available)
            val now = Instant.now()
            val timezone = ClockPreferences.getTimeZone()

            fun base() = SunTimes.compute().timezone(timezone).on(now).latitude(latitude).longitude(longitude)
            val times = TWILIGHTS.associateWith { base().twilight(it).execute() }
            val golden = base().twilight(GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE).execute()
            fun time(value: ZonedDateTime?) = value?.format(pattern) ?: notAvailable
            fun rise(twilight: SunTimes.Twilight) = times.getValue(twilight).rise
            fun set(twilight: SunTimes.Twilight) = times.getValue(twilight).set
            fun row(color: String, label: Int, value: ZonedDateTime?) =
                "<b><font color='$color'>$DOT</font> ${getString(label)}</b> ${time(value)}<br>"
            fun row(color: String, label: Int, from: ZonedDateTime?, to: ZonedDateTime?) =
                "<b><font color='$color'>$DOT</font> ${getString(label)}</b> ${time(from)} $ARROW ${time(to)}<br>"

            val twilightData = HtmlHelper.fromHtml(
                "<h3><b>${getString(R.string.twilight_morning)}</b></h3>" +
                row("#1a237e", R.string.twilight_astronomical_dusk, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL)) +
                row("#0d47a1", R.string.twilight_nautical_dusk, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL)) +
                row("#1565c0", R.string.twilight_blue_hour, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR)) +
                row("#1e88e5", R.string.twilight_civil_dusk, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)) +
                row("#42a5f5", R.string.twilight_visual, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL)) +
                row("#64b5f6", R.string.twilight_horizon, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)) +
                row("#90caf9", R.string.twilight_visual_lower, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER)) +
                row("#ffcc80", R.string.twilight_golden_hour, golden.rise, rise(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR)) +
                "<br><h3>${getString(R.string.twilight_evening)}</h3>" +
                row("#ffcc80", R.string.twilight_golden_hour, set(SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR), golden.set) +
                row("#90caf9", R.string.twilight_visual_lower, set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER)) +
                row("#64b5f6", R.string.twilight_horizon, set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON)) +
                row("#42a5f5", R.string.twilight_visual, set(SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL)) +
                row("#1e88e5", R.string.twilight_civil_dusk, set(SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON), set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL)) +
                row("#1565c0", R.string.twilight_blue_hour, set(SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR)) +
                row("#0d47a1", R.string.twilight_nautical_dusk, set(SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL)) +
                row("#1a237e", R.string.twilight_astronomical_dusk, set(SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL), set(SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL))
            )
            val updateTime = getString(R.string.last_updated) + " • " + pattern.format(ZonedDateTime.now())

            withContext(Dispatchers.Main) {
                val views = RemoteViews(packageName, R.layout.widget_twilight)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.twilight_info, twilightData)
                views.setTextViewText(R.id.widget_update_time, updateTime)
                AppWidgetManager.getInstance(this@updateWidget)
                    .updateAppWidget(ComponentName(this@updateWidget, TwilightWidget::class.java), views)
            }
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        const val DOT = "⬤"
        const val ARROW = "<b>~</b>"
        const val GOLDEN_HOUR_EDGE = -4.0
        val TWILIGHTS = listOf(
            SunTimes.Twilight.ASTRONOMICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.NAUTICAL, SunTimes.Twilight.CIVIL,
            SunTimes.Twilight.NIGHT_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.BLUE_HOUR, SunTimes.Twilight.HORIZON,
            SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL, SunTimes.Twilight.VISUAL_LOWER, SunTimes.Twilight.GOLDEN_HOUR,
        )
    }
}

<shortcuts xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android">
    <shortcut
        android:enabled="true"
        android:icon="@drawable/sc_01_clock"
        android:shortcutId="clock"
        android:shortcutLongLabel="@string/clock"
        android:shortcutShortLabel="@string/clock">
        <intent
            android:action="open_clock"
            android:targetClass="app.simple.positional.activities.main.LauncherActivity"
            android:targetPackage="app.simple.positional" />
    </shortcut>

    <shortcut
        android:enabled="true"
        android:icon="@drawable/sc_02_compass"

            android:action="open_compass"

            android:targetPackage="app.simple.positional" />
    </shortcut>

    <shortcut
        android:enabled="true"

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/constants/HtmlPageTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.constants

import app.simple.positional.R
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class HtmlPageTest {
    @Test
    fun everyPageHasAnAsset() {
        HtmlPage.entries.forEach { assertTrue(it.asset, File("src/main/assets/html/${it.asset}.html").isFile) }
    }

    @Test
    fun everyAssetHasAPage() {
        val assets = File("src/main/assets/html").listFiles().orEmpty().map { it.nameWithoutExtension }.toSet()
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.entries.map { it.asset }.toSet(), assets)
    }

    @Test
    fun legalTitlesResolve() {
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.PRIVACY_POLICY, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.privacy_policy))
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.TERMS, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.terms_of_use))
        assertEquals(HtmlPage.CREDITS, HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.credits))
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forTitle(R.string.app_name))
    }

    @Test
    fun namesRoundTrip() {
        HtmlPage.entries.forEach { assertEquals(it, HtmlPage.forName(it.name)) }
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forName(null))
        assertNull(HtmlPage.forName("unknown"))
    }

    @Test
    fun urlPointsToAndroidAssets() {
        assertEquals("file:///android_asset/html/credits.html", HtmlPage.CREDITS.url)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import app.simple.positional.math.Angle.normalizeEulerAngle
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class AngleTest {
    @Test
    fun normalizesEulerAngles() {
        assertEquals(10f, 370f.normalizeEulerAngle(false), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(270f, (-90f).normalizeEulerAngle(false), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(-90f, (-90f).normalizeEulerAngle(true), 1e-4f)
    }

    @Test
    fun touchAngleIsMeasuredClockwiseFromTop() {
        assertEquals(90f, Angle.getAngle(100.0, 50.0, 100f, 100f), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(180f, Angle.getAngle(50.0, 100.0, 100f, 100f), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(270f, Angle.getAngle(0.0, 50.0, 100f, 100f), 1e-3f)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class ClockMotionTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesStoredKeys() {
        ClockMotion.entries.forEach { assertEquals(it, ClockMotion.of(it.key)) }
        assertEquals(ClockMotion.TICK, ClockMotion.of("unknown"))
        assertEquals(ClockMotion.TICK, ClockMotion.of(null))
    }

    @Test
    fun onlyPhysicsModesAnimateSeconds() {
        assertTrue(ClockMotion.OSCILLATE.animated)
        assertTrue(ClockMotion.TICK_SMOOTH.animated)
        assertFalse(ClockMotion.TICK.fractional)
        assertEquals(0L, ClockMotion.SMOOTH.intervalMillis)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class CompassAzimuthTest {
    private val gravity = Vector3(0f, 0f, 9.81f)

    @Test
    fun deviceFacingNorth() {
        assertEquals(0f, CompassAzimuth.calculate(gravity, Vector3(0f, 20f, -40f)), 1e-3f)
    }

    @Test
    fun deviceFacingWest() {
        assertEquals(270f, CompassAzimuth.calculate(gravity, Vector3(20f, 0f, -40f)), 1e-3f)
    }

    @Test
    fun degenerateInputReturnsZero() {
        assertEquals(0f, CompassAzimuth.calculate(Vector3.zero, Vector3.zero), 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun inverseAddsHalfTurn() {
        assertEquals(190f, CompassAzimuth.inverse(10f), 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun adjustsForDisplayRotation() {
        assertEquals(100f, CompassAzimuth.adjustForRotation(100f, 0), 0f)
        assertEquals(-170f, CompassAzimuth.adjustForRotation(100f, 1), 0f)
        assertEquals(-80f, CompassAzimuth.adjustForRotation(100f, 2), 0f)
        assertEquals(10f, CompassAzimuth.adjustForRotation(100f, 3), 0f)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/GeoDistanceTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class GeoDistanceTest {
    @Test
    fun samePointIsZero() {
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.meters(37.0, -76.4, 37.0, -76.4), 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun oneDegreeOfLatitude() {
        assertEquals(111_195.0, GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 5.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun antipodesAreHalfCircumference() {
        assertEquals(20_015_115.0, GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 180.0), 50.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun distanceIsSymmetric() {
        val forward = GeoDistance.meters(51.5, -0.12, 48.85, 2.35)
        val backward = GeoDistance.meters(48.85, 2.35, 51.5, -0.12)
        assertEquals(forward, backward, 1e-6)
        assertEquals(343_500.0, forward, 1_500.0)
    }

    @Test
    fun thresholdIgnoresSmallMoves() {
        val threshold = MovementThreshold(25.0)
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(10.0, 10.0))
        assertFalse(threshold.accept(10.0001, 10.0))
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(10.001, 10.0))
        assertFalse(threshold.accept(10.001, 10.0))
    }

    @Test
    fun resetAcceptsAgain() {
        val threshold = MovementThreshold(25.0)
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(1.0, 1.0))
        threshold.reset()
        assertTrue(threshold.accept(1.0, 1.0))
    }

    @Test
    fun pathSumsSegments() {
        val a = 0.0 to 0.0
        val b = 1.0 to 0.0
        val c = 2.0 to 0.0
        assertEquals(GeoDistance.meters(0.0, 0.0, 2.0, 0.0), GeoDistance.path(listOf(a, b, c)), 1e-6)
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.path(listOf(a)), 0.0)
        assertEquals(0.0, GeoDistance.path(emptyList()), 0.0)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/LowPassFilterTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Test

class LowPassFilterTest {
    @Test
    fun blendsReadingsByAlpha() {
        val readings = floatArrayOf(0f, 10f, 20f)
        LowPassFilter.smoothAndSetReadings(readings, floatArrayOf(10f, 20f, 40f), 0.5f)
        assertArrayEquals(floatArrayOf(5f, 15f, 30f), readings, 1e-6f)
    }

    @Test
    fun alphaOneReplacesReadings() {
        val readings = floatArrayOf(1f, 2f, 3f)
        LowPassFilter.smoothAndSetReadings(readings, floatArrayOf(7f, 8f, 9f), 1f)
        assertArrayEquals(floatArrayOf(7f, 8f, 9f), readings, 0f)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/MathExtensionsTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.round
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.toDegrees
import app.simple.positional.math.MathExtensions.toNegative
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class MathExtensionsTest {
    @Test
    fun normalizeAngleWrapsIntoRange() {
        assertEquals(10f, MathExtensions.normalizeAngle(370f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(350f, MathExtensions.normalizeAngle(-10f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(0f, MathExtensions.normalizeAngle(720f), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(90.0, MathExtensions.normalizeAngle(-270.0), 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun trigonometryUsesDegrees() {
        assertEquals(1.0, MathExtensions.sinDegrees(90.0), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(-1.0, MathExtensions.cosDegrees(180.0), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(1.0, MathExtensions.tanDegrees(45.0), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(1f, MathExtensions.tanDegrees(45f), 1e-5f)
    }

    @Test
    fun clampLimitsValue() {
        assertEquals(5f, MathExtensions.clamp(10f, 0f, 5f), 0f)
        assertEquals(0f, MathExtensions.clamp(-3f, 0f, 5f), 0f)
        assertEquals(2.5f, MathExtensions.clamp(2.5f, 0f, 5f), 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun roundingAndConversions() {
        assertEquals(3.14, MathExtensions.round(3.14159, 2), 1e-12)
        assertTrue(MathExtensions.round(1.0, -1).isNaN())
        assertEquals(2.7f, 2.7182f.round(1), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(180.0, Math.PI.toDegrees(), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(90f, (Math.PI / 2).toFloat().toDegrees(), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(-4f, 4f.toNegative(), 0f)
        assertEquals(-4f, 4.toNegative(), 0f)
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/RotationPhysicsTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class RotationPhysicsTest {
    private fun settle(physics: RotationPhysics): Int {
        var frame = 0L
        var steps = 0
        while (physics.step(frame) && steps < 10_000) {
            frame += 16L
            steps++
        }
        return steps
    }

    @Test
    fun settlesOnTarget() {
        val physics = RotationPhysics()
        physics.snap(0f)
        physics.aim(90f)
        assertTrue(settle(physics) < 10_000)
        assertEquals(0.0, RotationPhysics.shortestDelta(physics.angle, 90.0), 0.2)
        assertTrue(physics.isSettled)
    }

    @Test
    fun takesShortestPathAcrossNorth() {
        val physics = RotationPhysics()
        physics.snap(350f)
        physics.aim(10f)
        physics.step(0L)
        assertTrue(physics.angle > 350.0)
        settle(physics)
        assertEquals(0.0, RotationPhysics.shortestDelta(physics.angle, 10.0), 0.2)
    }

    @Test
    fun snapStopsMotion() {
        val physics = RotationPhysics()
        physics.aim(120f)
        physics.snap(45f)
        assertEquals(45.0, physics.angle, 0.0)
        assertFalse(physics.step(0L))
    }

    @Test
    fun negativeCoefficientsFallBackToDefaults() {
        val physics = RotationPhysics(-1f, -1f, -1f)
        assertEquals(RotationPhysics.INERTIA, physics.inertia, 0f)
        assertEquals(RotationPhysics.DAMPING, physics.damping, 0f)
        assertEquals(RotationPhysics.MAGNETIC, physics.magnetic, 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun shortestDeltaWraps() {
        assertEquals(20.0, RotationPhysics.shortestDelta(350.0, 10.0), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(-20.0, RotationPhysics.shortestDelta(10.0, 350.0), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(0.0, RotationPhysics.shortestDelta(-360.0, 720.0), 1e-9)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals

class SeriesStatsTest {

    }

    @Test
    fun computesMinMaxAverage() {

        assertEquals(6.0, stats.max, 0.0)
        assertEquals(4.0, stats.average, 1e-9)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/TiltTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class TiltTest {
    private val gravity = floatArrayOf(1f, 2f, 9f)

    @Test
    fun remapsToScreenAxes() {
        assertEquals(Tilt(1f, 2f, 9f), Tilt.fromDevice(gravity, 0))
        assertEquals(Tilt(-2f, 1f, 9f), Tilt.fromDevice(gravity, 1))
        assertEquals(Tilt(-1f, -2f, 9f), Tilt.fromDevice(gravity, 2))
        assertEquals(Tilt(2f, -1f, 9f), Tilt.fromDevice(gravity, 3))
    }

    @Test
    fun flatDeviceIsLevel() {
        val tilt = Tilt(0.1f, -0.1f, 9.8f)
        assertTrue(tilt.isLevel)
        assertEquals(0f, Tilt.zero.horizontal, 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun uprightDeviceIsTilted() {
        val tilt = Tilt(0f, 9.8f, 0f)
        assertFalse(tilt.isLevel)
        assertEquals(90f, tilt.vertical, 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(0f, tilt.horizontal, 1e-3f)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals

import java.time.ZoneOffset
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

class TimeConverterTest {
    private val time = ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 1, 1, 15, 30, 15, 500_000_000, ZoneOffset.UTC)

    @Test
    fun convertsClockHandsToDegrees() {
        assertEquals(90f, TimeConverter.getSecondsInDegrees(time, false), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(93f, TimeConverter.getSecondsInDegrees(time, true), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(181.5f, TimeConverter.getMinutesInDegrees(time), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(465f, TimeConverter.getHoursInDegrees(time), 1e-3f)
        assertEquals(232.5f, TimeConverter.getHoursInDegreesFor24(time), 1e-3f)
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.math

import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toFeet
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toKiloMetersPerHour
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toKilometers
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toMiles
import app.simple.positional.math.UnitConverter.toMilesPerHour
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class UnitConverterTest {
    @Test
    fun convertsSpeeds() {
        assertEquals(36.0, 10.0.toKiloMetersPerHour(), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(36f, 10f.toKiloMetersPerHour(), 1e-4f)
        assertEquals(22.369, 10f.toMilesPerHour().toDouble(), 1e-2)
        assertEquals(62.1371, 100.0.toMilesPerHour(), 1e-9)
    }

    @Test
    fun convertsDistances() {
        assertEquals(1.5, 1500.0.toKilometers(), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(1.5f, 1500f.toKilometers(), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(1f, 1609f.toMiles(), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(6.21371, 10.0.toMiles(), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(328.084, 100.0.toFeet(), 1e-9)
        assertEquals(3.28084f, 1f.toFeet(), 1e-6f)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/math/Vector3Test.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.math

import org.junit.Assert.assertArrayEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class Vector3Test {
    private val x = Vector3(1f, 0f, 0f)
    private val y = Vector3(0f, 1f, 0f)

    @Test
    fun arithmetic() {
        assertEquals(Vector3(0f, 0f, 1f), x.cross(y))
        assertEquals(Vector3(1f, 1f, 0f), x + y)
        assertEquals(Vector3(1f, -1f, 0f), x - y)
        assertEquals(Vector3(3f, 0f, 0f), x * 3f)
        assertEquals(0f, x.dot(y), 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun magnitudeAndNormalize() {
        val v = Vector3(3f, 4f, 0f)
        assertEquals(5f, v.magnitude(), 1e-6f)
        assertEquals(1f, v.normalize().magnitude(), 1e-6f)
        assertArrayEquals(floatArrayOf(3f, 4f, 0f), Vector3.from(v.toFloatArray()).toFloatArray(), 0f)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/AccentPaletteTest.kt

package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class AccentPaletteTest {
    private val red = AccentOption(0xFFFF0000.toInt(), "Red")
    private val dynamic = AccentOption(0xFFFF0000.toInt(), AccentPalette.MATERIAL_YOU, dynamic = true)

    @Test
    fun staticColorSelectedByValue() {
        assertTrue(AccentPalette.isSelected(red, red.color, materialYou = false))
        assertFalse(AccentPalette.isSelected(red, 0xFF00FF00.toInt(), materialYou = false))
    }

    @Test
    fun dynamicSelectedOnlyWhenMaterialYou() {
        assertTrue(AccentPalette.isSelected(dynamic, 0, materialYou = true))
        assertFalse(AccentPalette.isSelected(red, red.color, materialYou = true))
        assertFalse(AccentPalette.isSelected(dynamic, dynamic.color, materialYou = false))
    }

    @Test
    fun paletteNamesAndColorsAreUnique() {
        assertEquals(23, AccentPalette.colors.size)
        assertEquals(AccentPalette.colors.size, AccentPalette.colors.map { it.first }.toSet().size)
        assertEquals(AccentPalette.colors.size, AccentPalette.colors.map { it.second }.toSet().size)
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/ComposeDrawablesTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class ComposeDrawablesTest {
    private val sources = listOf("src/main/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose", "src/main/java/app/simple/positional/constants", "src/main/java/app/simple/positional/util/MoonAngle.kt")
    private val reference = Regex("""R\.drawable\.(\w+)""")
    private val root = Regex("""<([a-zA-Z-]+)[\s>]""")

    @Test
    fun composeDrawablesAreVectorsOrBitmaps() {
        val names = sources.flatMap { path -> File(path).walk().filter { it.extension == "kt" }.toList() }
            .flatMap { file -> reference.findAll(file.readText()).map { it.groupValues[1] }.toList() }
            .toSet()
        assertTrue(names.isNotEmpty())
        val drawables = File("src/main/res").listFiles { file -> file.name.startsWith("drawable") }.orEmpty().flatMap { it.listFiles().orEmpty().toList() }
        names.forEach { name ->
            val files = drawables.filter { it.nameWithoutExtension == name }
            assertTrue(name, files.isNotEmpty())
            files.filter { it.extension == "xml" }.forEach { file ->
                val text = file.readText().replace(Regex("""<\?xml[^>]*>"""), "")
                assertTrue("$name is ${root.find(text)?.groupValues?.get(1)}", root.find(text)?.groupValues?.get(1) == "vector")
            }
        }
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/CoordinateInputTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class CoordinateInputTest {
    @Test
    fun parsesValidCoordinates() {
        assertEquals(12.5 to -45.25, CoordinateInput.parse("12.5", "-45.25"))
        assertEquals(90.0 to 180.0, CoordinateInput.parse(" 90 ", "180"))
        assertEquals(-90.0 to -180.0, CoordinateInput.parse("-90", "-180"))
    }

    @Test
    fun acceptsDecimalComma() {
        assertEquals(48.85 to 2.35, CoordinateInput.parse("48,85", "2,35"))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsOutOfRange() {
        assertNull(CoordinateInput.parse("90.01", "0"))
        assertNull(CoordinateInput.parse("0", "-180.5"))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsInvalidText() {
        assertNull(CoordinateInput.parse("", "10"))
        assertNull(CoordinateInput.parse("10", "abc"))
        assertNull(CoordinateInput.parse("NaN", "0"))
    }

    @Test
    fun formFillReplacesAllFields() {
        val form = CustomLocationForm()
        form.fill("Home", "1.0", "2.0")
        assertEquals("Home", form.address)
        assertEquals("1.0", form.latitude)
        assertEquals("2.0", form.longitude)
    }

    @Test
    fun formatsToSixDecimals() {
        assertEquals("48.858372", CoordinateInput.format(48.8583721))
        assertEquals("-2.5", CoordinateInput.format(-2.5))
        assertEquals("0.0", CoordinateInput.format(0.0))
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/DialogHostTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/MenuSheetStateTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import app.simple.positional.R
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class MenuSheetStateTest {
    @Test
    fun refreshRebuildsFromBuilder() {
        var checked = false
        val state = MenuSheetState()
        state.show { listOf(MenuItem.Toggle(R.string.labels, checked) { checked = it }) }
        val toggle = state.items!!.single() as MenuItem.Toggle
        toggle.onChange(!toggle.checked)
        state.refresh()
        assertEquals(true, (state.items!!.single() as MenuItem.Toggle).checked)
    }

    @Test
    fun dismissClearsItems() {
        val state = MenuSheetState()
        state.show { listOf(MenuItem.Action(R.string.help) {}) }
        state.dismiss()
        assertNull(state.items)
        state.refresh()
        assertNull(state.items)
    }
}

class PanelCatalogTest {
    @Test
    fun unknownTagsFallBackToLocation() {

        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.LOCATION, PanelCatalog.resolve("missing"))
        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.MEASURE, PanelCatalog.resolve("Measure"))
    }

    @Test
    fun stepWrapsAround() {
        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.COMPASS, PanelCatalog.step(PanelCatalog.CLOCK, 1))
        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.SETTINGS, PanelCatalog.step(PanelCatalog.CLOCK, -1))
        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.CLOCK, PanelCatalog.step(PanelCatalog.SETTINGS, 1))
    }

    @Test
    fun tagsAreUnique() {
        assertEquals(PanelCatalog.tags.size, PanelCatalog.tags.toSet().size)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/SettingsLabelsTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import app.simple.positional.util.NightMode
import app.simple.positional.R
import app.simple.positional.util.LocaleHelper
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class SettingsLabelsTest {
    @Test
    fun themeLabels() {
        assertEquals(R.string.theme_day, SettingsLabels.theme(NightMode.NO))
        assertEquals(R.string.theme_night, SettingsLabels.theme(NightMode.YES))
        assertEquals(R.string.theme_follow_system, SettingsLabels.theme(NightMode.FOLLOW_SYSTEM))
        assertEquals(R.string.theme_auto, SettingsLabels.theme(SettingsLabels.THEME_AUTO))
        assertNull(SettingsLabels.theme(99))
    }

    @Test
    fun unitLabels() {
        assertEquals(R.string.unit_metric, SettingsLabels.unit(true))
        assertEquals(R.string.unit_imperial, SettingsLabels.unit(false))
    }

    @Test
    fun coordinatesFormatLabels() {
        assertEquals(R.string.dd_dddddd, SettingsLabels.coordinatesFormat(0))
        assertEquals(R.string.dd_mm_mmm, SettingsLabels.coordinatesFormat(1))
        assertEquals(R.string.dd_mm_ss_sss, SettingsLabels.coordinatesFormat(2))
        assertNull(SettingsLabels.coordinatesFormat(3))
    }

    @Test
    fun locationProviderLabels() {
        assertEquals(R.string.android_location_provider, SettingsLabels.locationProvider(SettingsLabels.PROVIDER_ANDROID))
        assertEquals(R.string.fused_location_provider, SettingsLabels.locationProvider(SettingsLabels.PROVIDER_FUSED))
        assertNull(SettingsLabels.locationProvider("other"))
        assertEquals(R.string.desc_fused_location_provider, SettingsLabels.locationProviderDescription(SettingsLabels.PROVIDER_FUSED))
    }

    @Test
    fun everyChoiceHasALabel() {
        SettingsLabels.themeModes.forEach { assertNotNull(SettingsLabels.theme(it)) }
        SettingsLabels.coordinateFormats.forEach { assertNotNull(SettingsLabels.coordinatesFormat(it)) }
        SettingsLabels.providers.forEach {
            assertNotNull(SettingsLabels.locationProvider(it))
            assertNotNull(SettingsLabels.locationProviderDescription(it))
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun languagesStartWithDefaultThenSortByName() {
        val languages = SettingsLabels.languages()
        assertEquals(SettingsLabels.LANGUAGE_DEFAULT to null, languages.first())
        val names = languages.drop(1).map { it.second!! }
        assertEquals(names.sorted(), names)
        assertEquals(LocaleHelper.localeList.size, languages.size)
    }

    @Test
    fun languageNames() {
        assertNull(SettingsLabels.languageName(SettingsLabels.LANGUAGE_DEFAULT))
        assertEquals("Français", SettingsLabels.languageName("fr"))
        assertNull(SettingsLabels.languageName("xx"))
    }

    @Test
    fun legalPagesAreDistinct() {
        assertEquals(6, SettingsLabels.legalPages.toSet().size)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/SpeedometerDialTest.kt

package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import app.simple.positional.R
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class SpeedometerDialTest {
    @Test
    fun rangesSwitchEveryThreeHundredDegrees() {
        assertEquals(0, SpeedometerDial.range(0f))
        assertEquals(0, SpeedometerDial.range(300f))
        assertEquals(1, SpeedometerDial.range(300.5f))
        assertEquals(1, SpeedometerDial.range(600f))
        assertEquals(2, SpeedometerDial.range(900f))
        assertEquals(3, SpeedometerDial.range(901f))
    }

    @Test
    fun needleIsCompensatedPerRange() {
        assertEquals(120f, SpeedometerDial.needle(120f), 0f)
        assertEquals(340f, SpeedometerDial.needle(400f), 0f)
        assertEquals(580f, SpeedometerDial.needle(700f), 0f)
        assertEquals(820f, SpeedometerDial.needle(1000f), 0f)
    }

    @Test
    fun dialMatchesRange() {
        assertEquals(R.drawable.speedometer_dial_normal_range, SpeedometerDial.dial(10f))
        assertEquals(R.drawable.speedometer_dial_medium_range, SpeedometerDial.dial(450f))
        assertEquals(R.drawable.speedometer_dial_high_range, SpeedometerDial.dial(750f))
        assertEquals(R.drawable.speedometer_dial_max_range, SpeedometerDial.dial(2000f))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/ui/compose/TimeZoneCatalogTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.ui.compose

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.Instant
import java.util.Locale

class TimeZoneCatalogTest {
    private val now = Instant.parse("2026-01-15T12:30:00Z")
    private val entries = TimeZoneCatalog.entries(now, twelveHour = false, locale = Locale.US)

    @Test
    fun entriesAreSortedAndUnique() {
        val ids = entries.map { it.id }
        assertEquals(ids.sorted(), ids)
        assertEquals(ids.size, ids.toSet().size)
    }

    @Test
    fun utcUsesExplicitZeroOffset() {
        val utc = entries.first { it.id == "UTC" }
        assertEquals("+00:00", utc.offset)
        assertEquals("12:30", utc.time)
    }

    @Test
    fun twelveHourFormatUsesMeridiem() {
        val utc = TimeZoneCatalog.entries(now, twelveHour = true, locale = Locale.US).first { it.id == "UTC" }
        assertEquals("12:30 PM", utc.time)
    }

    @Test
    fun filterMatchesIdIgnoringCaseAndOffset() {
        assertTrue(TimeZoneCatalog.filter(entries, "  tokyo ").any { it.id == "Asia/Tokyo" })
        assertTrue(TimeZoneCatalog.filter(entries, "+09:00").all { it.offset.contains("+09:00") })
        assertEquals(entries, TimeZoneCatalog.filter(entries, "   "))
        assertTrue(TimeZoneCatalog.filter(entries, "no such zone").isEmpty())
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.ui.navigation

import app.simple.positional.constants.HtmlPage
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class RoutesTest {
    @Test
    fun webRouteMatchesTemplate() {
        HtmlPage.entries.forEach { page ->
            val route = Routes.web(page)
            assertEquals(Routes.WEB.replace("{${Routes.PAGE_ARGUMENT}}", page.name), route)
            assertEquals(page, HtmlPage.forName(route.removePrefix("web/")))
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun routesAreUnique() {
        val routes = listOf(
            Routes.PANELS, Routes.ACCENT, Routes.ARTS, Routes.TIMEZONE, Routes.MEASURES,
            Routes.TRAILS, Routes.DIRECTIONS, Routes.CUSTOM_LOCATIONS, Routes.MAP_SEARCH, Routes.WEB,
        )
        assertEquals(routes.size, routes.toSet().size)
        assertTrue(routes.none { it.contains(' ') })
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class AppIconsTest {
    @Test
    fun aliasesAndIconsAreUnique() {
        assertEquals(12, AppIcons.icons.size)
        assertEquals(AppIcons.icons.size, AppIcons.icons.map { it.first }.toSet().size)
        assertEquals(AppIcons.icons.size, AppIcons.icons.map { it.second }.toSet().size)
    }

    @Test
    fun componentNamesPointToAliasPackage() {
        AppIcons.icons.forEach { (alias, _) ->
            assertTrue(AppIcons.componentName(alias).startsWith("app.simple.positional.activities.alias."))
            assertTrue(AppIcons.componentName(alias).endsWith(alias))
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun manifestDeclaresEveryAlias() {
        val manifest = File("src/main/AndroidManifest.xml").readText()
        AppIcons.icons.forEach { (alias, _) -> assertTrue(alias, manifest.contains(".activities.alias.$alias\"")) }
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import app.simple.positional.util.ArrayHelper.isLastValueSame
import app.simple.positional.util.ArrayHelper.secondLastOrNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class ArrayHelperTest {
    @Test
    fun lastValueComparison() {
        assertFalse(arrayListOf<Float>().isLastValueSame(1f))
        assertTrue(arrayListOf(1f, 2f).isLastValueSame(2f))
        assertFalse(arrayListOf(1f, 2f).isLastValueSame(1f))
    }

    @Test
    fun secondLast() {
        assertNull(listOf(1).secondLastOrNull())
        assertEquals(2, listOf(1, 2, 3).secondLastOrNull())
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import app.simple.positional.R
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class DistanceTextTest {
    @Test
    fun metricSwitchesToKilometers() {
        assertEquals(999.5 to R.string.meter, DistanceText.parts(999.5, true))
        assertEquals(1.5 to R.string.kilometer, DistanceText.parts(1500.0, true))
    }

    @Test
    fun imperialSwitchesToMilesAtOneMile() {
        assertEquals(3280.84 to R.string.feet, DistanceText.parts(1000.0, false))
        assertEquals(2.0 to R.string.miles, DistanceText.parts(3218.688, false))
    }

    @Test
    fun zeroIsMeters() {
        assertEquals(0.0 to R.string.meter, DistanceText.parts(0.0, true))
    }
}

package app.simple.positional.util

import app.simple.positional.ui.compose.InfoRow
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class LocationShareTest {
    @Test
    fun shareTextRoundsAndLinks() {
        val text = LocationShare.text("Coordinates", 37.12345678, -76.98765432, "Somewhere")
        assertTrue(text.startsWith("Coordinates: 37.123457, -76.987654"))
        assertTrue(text.contains("query=37.123457,-76.987654"))
        assertTrue(text.contains("geo:37.123457,-76.987654?q=37.123457,-76.987654"))
        assertTrue(text.contains("Somewhere"))
    }

    @Test
    fun coordinateTextJoinsSections() {
        val text = CoordinateFormats.text(listOf("A" to listOf(InfoRow("x:", "1")), "B" to listOf(InfoRow("y"))))
        assertEquals("A\nx: 1\n\nB\ny", text)
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/util/NightModeTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class NightModeTest {
    @Test
    fun explicitModesIgnoreSystem() {
        assertTrue(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.YES, false, systemNight = false, powerSave = false, hour = 12))
        assertFalse(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.NO, false, systemNight = true, powerSave = true, hour = 23))
    }

    @Test
    fun followSystem() {
        assertTrue(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.FOLLOW_SYSTEM, false, systemNight = true, powerSave = false, hour = 12))
        assertFalse(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.FOLLOW_SYSTEM, false, systemNight = false, powerSave = true, hour = 23))
    }

    @Test
    fun batterySaverTurnsNightOn() {
        assertTrue(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.AUTO_BATTERY, false, systemNight = false, powerSave = true, hour = 12))
        assertFalse(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.AUTO_BATTERY, false, systemNight = false, powerSave = false, hour = 12))
    }

    @Test
    fun byTimeUsesSixToEighteenAsDay() {
        assertFalse(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.NO, true, systemNight = true, powerSave = false, hour = 6))
        assertFalse(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.AUTO_TIME, false, systemNight = true, powerSave = false, hour = 17))
        assertTrue(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.YES, true, systemNight = false, powerSave = false, hour = 18))
        assertTrue(NightMode.resolve(NightMode.AUTO_TIME, false, systemNight = false, powerSave = false, hour = 5))
    }
}

# FILE: app/src/test/java/app/simple/positional/util/TrailNamesTest.kt
################################################################################
package app.simple.positional.util

import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class TrailNamesTest {
    @Test
    fun acceptsNewNames() {
        assertTrue(TrailNames.isValid("Morning walk", listOf("Evening run")))
        assertTrue(TrailNames.isValid("  Hike  ", emptyList()))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsBlankReservedAndPathNames() {
        assertFalse(TrailNames.isValid("   ", emptyList()))
        assertFalse(TrailNames.isValid("%%_TRAILS", emptyList()))
        assertFalse(TrailNames.isValid("a/b", emptyList()))
        assertFalse(TrailNames.isValid("a\\b", emptyList()))
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsDuplicatesIgnoringCase() {
        assertFalse(TrailNames.isValid(" evening RUN ", listOf("Evening run")))
    }
}

################################################################################
# FILE: export.sh
################################################################################
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(dirname "$(readlink -f "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")")"
REPO_ROOT="$(git -C "$SCRIPT_DIR" rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null)" || exit 0
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

OUTPUT_DIR="docs/llm"
OUTPUT_FILE="$OUTPUT_DIR/dump.txt"
SKIP='^(docs/llm/|gradlew$|gradlew\.bat$|gradle/wrapper/|screenshots/|LICENSE$|CODE_OF_CONDUCT\.md$|app/src/[^/]+/res/(drawable[^/]*|mipmap[^/]*|font|raw)/|app/src/main/res/values-[^/]+/strings\.xml$|app/src/main/assets/(html|css|fonts|svg)/|app/databases/schemas/)'

mapfile -t FILES < <(git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard | grep -Ev '^docs/llm/' | LC_ALL=C sort -u)

mkdir -p "$OUTPUT_DIR"
TMP_FILE="$(mktemp "$OUTPUT_DIR/.dump.XXXXXX")"
trap 'rm -f "$TMP_FILE"' EXIT

{
    echo "# REPOSITORY CONTEXT DUMP"
    echo "Generated: $(date --iso-8601=seconds)"
    echo "Branch:    $(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo none)"
    echo "Commit:    $(git log -1 --pretty='%h %ci %s' 2>/dev/null || echo none)"
    echo "Remote:    $(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo none)"
    echo
    echo "## STATUS"
    git status --short | grep -v ' docs/llm/' || true
    echo
    echo "## TREE (${#FILES[@]} files)"
    printf '%s\n' "${FILES[@]}" | awk -F/ '{
        same = 1
        for (i = 1; i < NF; i++) {
            if (same && prev[i] == $i) continue
            same = 0
            printf "%*s%s/\n", (i - 1) * 2, "", $i
        }
        printf "%*s%s\n", (NF - 1) * 2, "", $NF
        for (i = 1; i < NF; i++) prev[i] = $i
        for (i = NF; i in prev; i++) delete prev[i]
    }'
    for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
        [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
        [[ "$f" =~ $SKIP ]] && continue
        [[ ! -s "$f" ]] || grep -Iq . "$f" || continue
        echo
        echo "################################################################################"
        echo "# FILE: $f"
        echo "################################################################################"
        cat "$f"
        [[ -z "$(tail -c1 "$f")" ]] || echo
    done
} > "$TMP_FILE"

mv -f "$TMP_FILE" "$OUTPUT_FILE"
trap - EXIT
echo "$OUTPUT_FILE: $(wc -c < "$OUTPUT_FILE") bytes"

################################################################################
